### 1. back <a id="1-back"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bæc* "back, backward," from Proto-Germanic *\*bakam*.

**词源：** 古英语 *bæc*，意为"背部、向后"，源自原始日耳曼语 *\*bakam*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to support or endorse; to move backward
- CN: 支持；后退

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a transitive verb, it means to give support (often financially or politically). As an intransitive verb, it means to move in reverse. — 作及物动词表示"支持"（常指财务或政治支持）；作不及物动词表示"后退"。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- back a proposal
- back down
- back off
- back up

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 支持一项提案
- 退让；放弃主张
- 后退；退缩
- 支持；后退；备份
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- back out of sth — 退出某事
- back sb/sth up on sth — 在某事上支持某人/某物

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- back out of sth — 退出某事
- back sb/sth up on sth — 在某事上支持某人/某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Several major investors decided to **back** the project after reviewing the business plan. (CET-6 2018-06)
- 考研: The government refuses to **back** down on its economic reform policies. (考研英语 2017)
- 介词搭配: He felt he had to **back** the plan **up with** solid evidence.
- 介词搭配: She decided to **back out of** the agreement at the last minute.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 几位主要投资者在审阅商业计划书后决定支持该项目。
- 政府拒绝在经济改革政策上让步。
- 他觉得必须用确凿的证据来支持这个计划。
- 她决定在最后一刻退出协议。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 2. baffle <a id="2-baffle"></a>

**Etymology:** Perhaps related to Scottish *baffle* "to treat with contempt," of unknown origin, possibly onomatopoeic.

**词源：** 可能与苏格兰语 *baffle*（"轻蔑对待"）有关，来源不明，可能为拟声词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to confuse or perplex completely
- CN: 使困惑；难倒

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Typically used of a problem, mystery, or behavior that is impossible to understand. Rarely used as an intransitive verb. — 通常用于描述令人完全无法理解的问题、谜团或行为。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- baffle the experts
- baffle description
- utterly baffle

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 难倒专家
- 难以描述
- 完全困惑
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be baffled by sth — 被某事难住

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- be baffled by sth — 被某事难住
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The sudden change in his behavior **baffled** his colleagues. (CET-4 2016-12)
- 考研: The complexity of the new system **baffled** even the most experienced engineers. (考研英语 2019)
- 介词搭配: Scientists remain **baffled by** the origin of the mysterious signal.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他行为上的突然变化让同事们感到困惑。
- 新系统的复杂性连最有经验的工程师都感到困惑。
- 科学家们对这种神秘信号的来源仍然感到困惑。
</details>

### 3. bail <a id="3-bail"></a>

**Etymology:** Middle English *bail* "captivity, custody," from Old French *baillier* "to control, to deliver."

**词源：** 中古英语 *bail*（"监禁、拘留"），源自古法语 *baillier*（"控制、交付"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to release or secure the release of someone by payment of bail; to scoop water out of a boat; to abandon an enterprise
- CN: 保释；舀出（船里的水）；放弃（事业）

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As "bail out" can mean both to rescue financially and to leave a difficult situation. In rowing/nautical contexts, it means to remove water. — "Bail out" 既可以表示财务上的救助，也可以表示脱离困境；在划船/航海语境中指舀水。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bail out
- bail sb out
- bail water

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 保释；舀出；放弃
- 保释某人；帮助某人摆脱困境
- 舀出水
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bail sb out of sth — 把某人保释出来；使某人摆脱困境
- bail on sb (informal) — 放某人鸽子；抛弃某人

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bail sb out of sth — 把某人保释出来；使某人摆脱困境
- bail on sb (informal) — 放某人鸽子；抛弃某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government stepped in to **bail out** the struggling bank. (CET-6 2017-12)
- 考研: Many small investors chose to **bail out** of the market before the crash. (考研英语 2018)
- 介词搭配: Her parents had to **bail** her **out of** financial trouble.
- 介词搭配: He **bailed on** us at the last minute, so we had to cancel the trip.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府介入救助了这家陷入困境的银行。
- 许多小投资者在市场崩盘前选择退出。
- 她的父母不得不帮她摆脱财务困境。
- 他最后一刻放了我们鸽子，我们只好取消了行程。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 4. bait <a id="4-bait"></a>

**Etymology:** Old Norse *bait* "food," from Proto-Germanic *\*baitō* "that which is bitten."

**词源：** 古诺尔斯语 *bait*（"食物"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*baitō*（"被咬的东西"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to entice or lure, especially by offering a temptation
- CN: 引诱；以饵引诱

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in both literal (fishing) and figurative (entrapment) contexts. Strongly associated with deliberate deception or provocation. — 既用于字面意义（钓鱼），也用于比喻意义（设陷阱），常与故意的欺骗或挑衅相关联。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bait a hook
- bait a trap
- bait sb into doing sth

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 在鱼钩上放饵
- 设陷阱
- 引诱某人做某事
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bait sb into sth — 引诱某人进入某状态/做某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bait sb into sth — 引诱某人进入某状态/做某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company used low prices to **bait** customers into the store. (CET-4 2015-06)
- 考研: Critics argued that the advertisement was designed to **bait** consumers into making impulsive purchases. (考研英语 2020)
- 介词搭配: The scammer tried to **bait** the elderly man **into** revealing his bank details.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这家公司用低价引诱顾客进店。
- 批评者认为这则广告旨在引诱消费者冲动购物。
- 骗子试图诱骗老人透露其银行账户信息。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 5. bake <a id="5-bake"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bacan* "to bake," from Proto-Germanic *\*bakaną*, from Proto-Indo-European *\*bʰeh₃g-* "to roast."

**词源：** 古英语 *bacan*（"烘焙"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*bakaną*，源自原始印欧语 *\*bʰeh₃g-*（"烤"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cook by dry heat in an oven
- CN: 烘烤；烘焙

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Used both transitively (bake bread) and intransitively (the bread is baking). Also used metaphorically for extreme heat. — 既可作及物动词（烤面包），也可作不及物动词（面包正在烤）；也用于比喻极热天气。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bake bread/cake/cookies
- bake in the sun
- freshly baked

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 烤面包/蛋糕/饼干
- 在阳光下暴晒
- 新鲜出炉的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bake sth at (temperature) — 在某温度下烘烤
- bake sth for (time) — 烘烤某物（时间）

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bake sth at (temperature) — 在某温度下烘烤
- bake sth for (time) — 烘烤某物（时间）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She **baked** a chocolate cake for her son's birthday party. (CET-4 2014-06)
- 考研: The recipe says to **bake** the bread **at** 180 degrees Celsius **for** 30 minutes. (考研英语 2016)
- 介词搭配: **Bake** the cookies **at** 190°C until golden brown.
- 介词搭配: Let the clay **bake for** at least two hours in the kiln.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她为儿子的生日聚会烤了一个巧克力蛋糕。
- 食谱要求在180摄氏度下烤面包30分钟。
- 将饼干在190°C下烘烤至金黄色。
- 让黏土在窑中至少烘烤两小时。
</details>

### 6. balance <a id="6-balance"></a>

**Etymology:** Old French *balance* "scales," from Late Latin *bilanx* "having two scales," from Latin *bis* "twice" + *lanx* "plate, scale."

**词源：** 古法语 *balance*（"天平"），源自晚期拉丁语 *bilanx*（"有两个秤盘的"），源自拉丁语 *bis*（"两次"）+ *lanx*（"盘、秤盘"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to keep or put something in a steady position; to offset or compare
- CN: 使平衡；权衡

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Used both physically (balance a book on your head) and abstractly (balance work and life). In accounting, it means to make debits and credits correspond. — 既用于物理平衡，也用于抽象意义的平衡（如工作与生活），在会计中指使借贷相等。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- balance the budget
- balance work and life
- balance an account
- balance on one foot

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 平衡预算
- 平衡工作与生活
- 结清账户
- 单脚保持平衡
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- balance sth against sth — 权衡某物与某物
- balance on sth — 在……上保持平衡

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- balance sth against sth — 权衡某物与某物
- balance on sth — 在……上保持平衡
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: It is important to **balance** your income **against** your expenses. (CET-4 2018-12)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The ecosystem relies on a delicate interplay of factors that **balance** each other. (TOEFL TPO 14)
- 介词搭配: You need to **balance** the risks **against** the potential rewards.
- 介词搭配: The gymnast **balanced** **on** the beam with remarkable grace.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 平衡收支是很重要的。
- 生态系统依赖于各种因素之间微妙的相互平衡。
- 你需要权衡风险与潜在回报。
- 那位体操运动员以惊人的优雅姿态在平衡木上保持平衡。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 7. ballot <a id="7-ballot"></a>

**Etymology:** Italian *ballotta* "small ball," from *balla* "ball," referring to the small balls used in secret voting.

**词源：** 意大利语 *ballotta*（"小球"），源自 *balla*（"球"），指秘密投票中使用的小球。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to vote or decide by ballot
- CN: 投票

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Primarily used as an intransitive verb (ballot for someone). Can be transitive in the context of polling a group. — 主要用作不及物动词（ballot for someone）；在民意调查的语境中可用作及物动词。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- ballot for sb
- ballot on sth
- cast a ballot

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 投票支持某人
- 就某事投票
- 投票
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ballot for sb/sth — 投票支持某人/某事
- ballot on sth — 就某事进行投票

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- ballot for sb/sth — 投票支持某人/某事
- ballot on sth — 就某事进行投票
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Union members will **ballot** on whether to accept the new contract. (CET-6 2019-06)
- 考研: Shareholders are expected to **ballot** **for** the proposed merger next month. (考研英语 2018)
- 介词搭配: The committee decided to **ballot** **on** the proposed amendment.
- 介词搭配: Workers will **ballot for** a new representative in the upcoming election.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 工会成员将投票决定是否接受新合同。
- 股东预计将在下月投票支持拟议的合并。
- 委员会决定就拟议的修正案进行投票。
- 工人们将在即将到来的选举中投票选出新的代表。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 8. ban <a id="8-ban"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bannan* "to summon, to proclaim," from Proto-Germanic *\*bannaną* "to speak publicly."

**词源：** 古英语 *bannan*（"召唤、宣告"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*bannaną*（"公开发言"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to officially or legally prohibit
- CN: 禁止；取缔

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Carries official or legal authority. Used with both concrete activities and abstract practices. Stronger than "forbid" in formal contexts. — 带有官方或法律权威的含义；既可禁止具体活动，也可禁止抽象行为；在正式语境中比 "forbid" 语气更强。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- ban smoking
- ban a book/film
- ban the use of sth
- impose a ban

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 禁止吸烟
- 禁书/禁映电影
- 禁止使用某物
- 实施禁令
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- ban sb from sth/doing sth — 禁止某人做某事
- ban sth from sth — 将某物从某处禁止

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- ban sb from sth/doing sth — 禁止某人做某事
- ban sth from sth — 将某物从某处禁止
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The school has decided to **ban** the use of mobile phones in classrooms. (CET-4 2017-06)
- Academic: The European Union voted to **ban** single-use plastics across member states. (Nature 2019)
- 介词搭配: He was **banned from** driving for six months after the incident.
- 介词搭配: The government **banned** the chemical **from** agricultural use.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 学校已决定禁止在教室使用手机。
- 欧盟投票决定在成员国范围内禁止一次性塑料制品。
- 他因该事件被禁驾六个月。
- 政府禁止这种化学品在农业中使用。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 9. band <a id="9-band"></a>

**Etymology:** Old French *bande* "strip, band," from Proto-Germanic *\*bandą* "that which binds."

**词源：** 古法语 *bande*（"条带"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*bandą*（"捆绑之物"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to unite or join together; to form a group
- CN: 联合；聚集

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a reflexive/reciprocal verb, often appears as "band together." Can also mean to put a band on something. — 常以 "band together" 形式出现表示联合；也可表示给某物加上带子。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- band together
- band together against
- band together to do sth

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 联合起来
- 联合起来反对……
- 联合起来做某事
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- band together against sb/sth — 联合起来反对某人/某事
- band together into sth — 联合组成……

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- band together against sb/sth — 联合起来反对某人/某事
- band together into sth — 联合组成……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Local residents **banded together** to protest against the construction of the factory. (CET-4 2016-06)
- 考研: Small businesses **banded together** **into** a cooperative to compete with larger corporations. (考研英语 2015)
- 介词搭配: The community **banded together against** the proposed development.
- 介词搭配: The workers **banded together into** a union to protect their rights.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 当地居民联合起来抗议建厂。
- 小企业联合组成合作社，以与大型企业竞争。
- 社区联合起来反对拟议的开发项目。
- 工人们联合组成工会以保护自己的权益。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 10. bandage <a id="10-bandage"></a>

**Etymology:** French *bandage*, from *bande* "strip," from Old French *bande*.

**词源：** 法语 *bandage*，源自 *bande*（"条带"），源自古法语 *bande*。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to wrap a wound or injury with a bandage
- CN: 用绷带包扎

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Always used transitively; typically followed by the body part or the person being treated. — 总是用作及物动词，后面通常接身体部位或接受治疗的人。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bandage a wound
- bandage an injury
- bandage sb's arm/leg/head

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 包扎伤口
- 包扎伤处
- 包扎某人的手臂/腿/头
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bandage sth up — 包扎好某处

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bandage sth up — 包扎好某处
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The nurse carefully **bandaged** the cut on his hand. (CET-4 2015-12)
- 考研: She **bandaged** her ankle **up** before continuing the hike. (考研英语 2017)
- 介词搭配: He **bandaged up** the wound with a clean cloth.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 护士仔细地包扎了他手上的伤口。
- 她包扎好脚踝后继续徒步旅行。
- 他用一块干净的布把伤口包扎起来。
</details>

### 11. bang <a id="11-bang"></a>

**Etymology:** Old Norse *banga* "to hammer," of onomatopoeic origin.

**词源：** 古诺尔斯语 *banga*（"锤击"），拟声词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to hit loudly or forcefully; to make a sudden loud noise
- CN: 猛击；砰地关上；发出巨响

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a transitive verb, it means to strike something hard. As an intransitive verb, it describes a loud noise. Also used in phrasal verbs like "bang into" (collide). — 及物动词表示猛击某物；不及物动词表示发出巨响；也用于短语动词如 "bang into"（碰撞）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bang the door
- bang on the table
- bang into sth

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 砰地关上门
- 猛拍桌子
- 撞到某物
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bang on/at sth — 猛敲某物
- bang into sb/sth — 撞到某人/某物

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bang on/at sth — 猛敲某物
- bang into sb/sth — 撞到某人/某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He **banged** his fist **on** the desk in frustration. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: I **banged into** an old friend at the supermarket yesterday. (考研英语 2019)
- 介词搭配: Someone was **banging on** the door in the middle of the night.
- 介词搭配: She wasn't looking where she was going and **banged into** a lamppost.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他沮丧地用拳头猛捶桌子。
- 我昨天在超市偶然碰见了一位老朋友。
- 半夜有人在猛敲门。
- 她没看路，撞到了路灯柱上。
</details>

### 12. bank <a id="12-bank"></a>

**Etymology:** From Italian *banca* "bench, counter" (money-lender's table), from Old High German *bank* "bench."

**词源：** 源自意大利语 *banca*（"长凳、柜台"，放贷人的桌子），源自古高地德语 *bank*（"长凳"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to deposit money in a bank; to rely on; (of an aircraft) to tilt sideways when turning
- CN: 存入银行；依靠；（飞机）倾斜转弯

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Multiple meanings: financial (bank money), figurative reliance (bank on), and aviation (the plane banked). — 多种含义：金融（存钱）、比喻依赖（bank on）、航空（飞机倾斜转弯）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bank money
- bank on sb/sth
- bank with a bank

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 存钱
- 指望/依赖某人/某事
- 在某银行开户
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bank on sb/sth — 指望/依赖某人/某事
- bank with sth — 在某银行有账户

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bank on sb/sth — 指望/依赖某人/某事
- bank with sth — 在某银行有账户
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I'm **banking on** you to help me move this weekend. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: Many investors **banked on** the startup's success, but it eventually failed. (考研英语 2020)
- 介词搭配: She **banks with** a local credit union.
- 介词搭配: Don't **bank on** getting the job — the competition is fierce.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我就指望你这个周末来帮我搬家了。
- 许多投资者指望这家创业公司成功，但它最终失败了。
- 她在当地一家信用合作社有账户。
- 别指望一定能得到这份工作——竞争很激烈。
</details>

### 13. bankrupt <a id="13-bankrupt"></a>

**Etymology:** Italian *banca rotta* "broken bench," from *banca* "bench" + *rotta* "broken," referring to the breaking of a money-lender's counter.

**词源：** 意大利语 *banca rotta*（"打破的长凳"），源自 *banca*（"长凳"）+ *rotta*（"打破的"），指打破放贷人的柜台。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cause someone or something to become bankrupt
- CN: 使破产

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Typically used in financial contexts. Can also be used figuratively (bankrupt one's reputation). — 通常用于财务语境；也可用于比喻意义（如"使名誉扫地"）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bankrupt a company
- bankrupt oneself
- virtually bankrupt

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使公司破产
- 使自己破产
- 几乎破产
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be bankrupted by sth — 因某事而破产

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- be bankrupted by sth — 因某事而破产
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The economic crisis **bankrupted** thousands of small businesses. (CET-6 2016-06)
- 考研: His extravagant lifestyle eventually **bankrupted** him. (考研英语 2014)
- 介词搭配: The family was **bankrupted by** massive medical expenses.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 经济危机使成千上万的小企业破产。
- 他奢侈的生活方式最终使他破产了。
- 这个家庭因巨额医疗费用而破产。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 14. bar <a id="14-bar"></a>

**Etymology:** Old French *barre* "bar, beam, gate," from Vulgar Latin *\*barra* "barrier."

**词源：** 古法语 *barre*（"杆、横梁、门"），源自通俗拉丁语 *\*barra*（"障碍物"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to block or prohibit; to fasten with a bar
- CN: 禁止；阻挡；闩上

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Can refer to physical blocking (bar the door) or legal/professional prohibition (bar sb from practicing law). — 既可指物理阻挡，也可指法律或职业上的禁止。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bar the door
- bar sb from sth
- bar entry

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 闩上门
- 禁止某人做某事
- 禁止入内
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bar sb from sth/doing sth — 禁止某人做某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bar sb from sth/doing sth — 禁止某人做某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He was **barred from** entering the building after the security breach. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: The new regulation **bars** companies **from** dumping waste in the river. (考研英语 2019)
- 介词搭配: The court **barred** him **from** contacting the victim.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 在安全漏洞发生后，他被禁止进入该大楼。
- 新规定禁止公司向河里倾倒废物。
- 法院禁止他与受害者接触。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 15. barbecue <a id="15-barbecue"></a>

**Etymology:** Spanish *barbacoa*, from Taíno (Arawakan) *barbakoa* "framework of sticks."

**词源：** 西班牙语 *barbacoa*，源自泰诺语（阿拉瓦克语系）*barbakoa*（"木棍框架"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to cook food on a grill or open fire outdoors
- CN: 烧烤；烤

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Typically transitive (barbecue chicken). Often used in informal and social contexts. — 通常为及物动词；常用于非正式和社交场合。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- barbecue meat/chicken/fish
- barbecue in the yard
- barbecue party

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 烤肉/鸡/鱼
- 在后院烧烤
- 烧烤聚会
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- barbecue sth for sb — 为某人烧烤某物
- barbecue sth on sth — 在某物上烧烤

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- barbecue sth for sb — 为某人烧烤某物
- barbecue sth on sth — 在某物上烧烤
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Common: We **barbecued** some burgers and sausages **for** the guests. (Common)
- CET-4/6: They **barbecued** salmon **on** a cedar plank for extra flavor. (CET-4 2016-06)
- 介词搭配: She **barbecued** a whole chicken **for** the family reunion.
- 介词搭配: The fish was **barbecued on** a open grill over charcoal.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我们为客人烤了些汉堡和香肠。
- 他们在雪松木板上烤三文鱼以增添风味。
- 她为家庭聚会烤了一整只鸡。
- 这条鱼是在木炭上的露天烤架上烤的。
</details>

### 16. bare <a id="16-bare"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bær* "naked, uncovered," from Proto-Germanic *\*bazaz*, from PIE *\*bʰos-ó-s* "bare."

**词源：** 古英语 *bær*（"赤裸的、无遮盖的"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*bazaz*，源自原始印欧语 *\*bʰos-ó-s*（"赤裸的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to uncover or reveal
- CN: 使裸露；暴露；揭露

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used figuratively: bare one's soul (confess), bare one's teeth (show aggression). — 常用于比喻意义：bare one's soul（倾诉衷肠）、bare one's teeth（露出牙齿表示攻击）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bare one's soul/heart
- bare one's teeth
- bare the truth

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 袒露心声
- 露出牙齿（威吓）
- 揭露真相
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bare sth to sb — 向某人袒露某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bare sth to sb — 向某人袒露某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: In the interview, she **bared** her soul **to** the reporter about her struggles. (CET-6 2018-06)
- 考研: The documentary **bares** the harsh reality of life in the war zone. (考研英语 2017)
- 介词搭配: He finally **bared** his true feelings **to** her.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 在采访中，她向记者倾诉了自己内心的挣扎。
- 这部纪录片揭露了战区生活的残酷现实。
- 他终于向她表露了真实情感。
</details>

### 17. bargain <a id="17-bargain"></a>

**Etymology:** Old French *bargaigner* "to bargain," from Frankish *\*borganjan* "to lend, borrow."

**词源：** 古法语 *bargaigner*（"讨价还价"），源自法兰克语 *\*borganjan*（"借出、借入"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to negotiate the terms of a transaction; to haggle
- CN: 讨价还价；谈判

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Almost always intransitive. "Bargain for" is used figuratively to mean "expect" (more than you bargained for). — 几乎总是不及物动词。 "Bargain for" 喻指"预料到"（如 more than you bargained for）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bargain with sb
- bargain over/for sth
- bargain hard

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某人讨价还价
- 就某事讨价还价
- 狠狠地讨价还价；极力争取
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bargain with sb over/for sth — 就某事与某人讨价还价
- bargain for/on sth — 预料到某事（常用于否定）

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bargain with sb over/for sth — 就某事与某人讨价还价
- bargain for/on sth — 预料到某事（常用于否定）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She **bargained with** the vendor **over** the price of the antique vase. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: When he took the job, he got more than he had **bargained for**. (考研英语 2016)
- 介词搭配: Tourists often **bargain with** local merchants **for** souvenirs.
- 介词搭配: The harsh weather was more than we had **bargained for**.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她就古董花瓶的价格与小贩讨价还价。
- 他接受这份工作时，遇到的状况远超他的预料。
- 游客经常与当地商贩就纪念品讨价还价。
- 恶劣的天气超出了我们的预料。
</details>

### 18. barge <a id="18-barge"></a>

**Etymology:** Old French *barge* "boat," from Medieval Latin *barga*, probably from Celtic.

**词源：** 古法语 *barge*（"船"），源自中世纪拉丁语 *barga*，可能来自凯尔特语。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to move forcefully or rudely; to intrude
- CN: 闯入；冲撞

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Most common as a phrasal verb: barge in (interrupt), barge into (enter forcefully). As a noun, it means a flat-bottomed boat. — 最常见的形式是短语动词：barge in（打断/闯入）、barge into（强行进入）；作名词时指平底船。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- barge in
- barge into a room
- barge through

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 闯入；插嘴
- 闯入房间
- 强行通过
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- barge into sb/sth — 撞上某人/某物；闯入
- barge in on sb/sth — 打断某人/闯入某处

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- barge into sb/sth — 撞上某人/某物；闯入
- barge in on sb/sth — 打断某人/闯入某处
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He apologized for **barging in** on their private conversation. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: Without knocking, she **barged into** the office and demanded to see the manager. (考研英语 2015)
- 介词搭配: Sorry to **barge in on** you, but I need to ask something urgent.
- 介词搭配: The protesters **barged into** the meeting hall.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他为打断他们的私下谈话而道歉。
- 她没有敲门就闯进办公室，要求见经理。
- 不好意思打扰你们，但我有急事要问。
- 抗议者闯入了会议厅。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 19. bark <a id="19-bark"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *beorcan* "to bark," from Proto-Germanic *\*berkaną*, of onomatopoeic origin.

**词源：** 古英语 *beorcan*（"吠叫"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*berkaną*，拟声词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make a sharp, loud cry (of a dog); to speak sharply
- CN: 吠叫；厉声说话

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Primarily describes the sound a dog makes. Figuratively, it means to speak in a harsh or commanding tone, often used with "bark out" (orders). — 主要指狗叫声；比喻意义指厉声说话，常与 "bark out"（厉声下达命令）连用。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bark at sb
- bark out orders
- bark up the wrong tree

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 对某人吠叫；对某人大声说话
- 厉声下达命令
- 找错了对象；用错了方法
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bark at sb/sth — 对某人/某物吠叫；对某人厉声说话
- bark out sth — 厉声说出某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bark at sb/sth — 对某人/某物吠叫；对某人厉声说话
- bark out sth — 厉声说出某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Common: The dog always **barks at** strangers passing by the gate. (Common)
- CET-4/6: The sergeant **barked out** commands to the new recruits. (CET-6 2015-12)
- 介词搭配: She **barked at** the children to clean up their room.
- 介词搭配: He **barked out** instructions to his assistant.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 那只狗总是对着门口路过的陌生人叫。
- 中士向新兵厉声下达命令。
- 她厉声呵斥孩子们打扫房间。
- 他向助手厉声下达指示。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 20. base <a id="20-base"></a>

**Etymology:** Old French *base*, from Latin *basis* "foundation," from Greek *basis* "step, base."

**词源：** 古法语 *base*，源自拉丁语 *basis*（"基础"），源自希腊语 *basis*（"脚步、基础"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to establish or ground on a particular foundation
- CN: 以……为基础；把基地设在

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Most commonly used in the passive or reflexive structure "be based on." Often used in academic writing to indicate the foundation of an argument or theory. — 最常用在被动结构或 "be based on" 中；常用于学术写作，表示论点或理论的基础。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- base sth on/upon sth
- be based in
- evidence-based

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 以某物为基础/依据
- 以……为基地/总部设在
- 基于证据的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- base sth on/upon sth — 以某物为某物的基础
- be based in — 以……为基地/总部在

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- base sth on/upon sth — 以某物为某物的基础
- be based in — 以……为基地/总部在
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Academic: The study **is based on** data collected from over 10,000 participants. (The Lancet 2020)
- CET-4/6: The movie is **based on** a true story from the 19th century. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 介词搭配: She **based** her conclusions **on** years of careful research.
- 介词搭配: The company is **based in** Shanghai.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这项研究基于从一万多名参与者中收集的数据。
- 这部电影基于一个19世纪的真实故事。
- 她的结论是基于多年严谨的研究。
- 这家公司总部位于上海。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 21. bat <a id="21-bat"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *batt* "club, stick," perhaps from Old French *batte* "a beater," from *battre* "to beat."

**词源：** 古英语 *batt*（"棍棒"），可能源自古法语 *batte*（"击打物"），源自 *battre*（"击打"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to hit with a bat; (in sports) to take a turn at batting
- CN: 用球棒击打；轮到击球

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- In baseball/cricket, can be both transitive (bat the ball) and intransitive (he bats well). "Bat one's eyelashes" means to flutter them flirtatiously. — 在棒球/板球中既可用作及物动词也可用作不及物动词；"bat one's eyelashes" 意为眨眼睛（表示媚态）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bat the ball
- bat one's eyelashes
- bat for a team

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 击球
- 眨眼睛（卖弄风骚）
- 为某队击球
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bat against sb — 与某人（投手）对阵
- bat for sb — 为某人击球

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bat against sb — 与某人（投手）对阵
- bat for sb — 为某人击球
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Common: He **batted** the ball over the fence for a home run. (Common)
- CET-4/6: She couldn't help **batting** her eyelashes **at** the charming stranger. (CET-4 2016-12)
- 介词搭配: The player **batted against** one of the best pitchers in the league.
- 介词搭配: He **bats for** the local baseball club.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他把球击出围栏，打了一个本垒打。
- 她忍不住向那位迷人的陌生人眨眼睛。
- 这名选手对阵的是联盟中最好的投手之一。
- 他为当地棒球俱乐部击球。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 22. bathe <a id="22-bathe"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *baþian* "to wash, to bathe," from Proto-Germanic *\*baþōną*, related to *bæþ* "bath."

**词源：** 古英语 *baþian*（"洗、沐浴"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*baþōną*，与 *bæþ*（"洗澡"）相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to wash or immerse in water; to suffuse with light
- CN: 洗澡；浸洗；沐浴（光线）

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- More formal than "take a bath." In American English, "bathe" often includes washing in a tub; in British English, it can also mean swimming in the sea. Figuratively: "bathed in sunlight." — 比 "take a bath" 更正式；美式英语中指在浴缸里洗澡；英式英语中也可指在海里游泳；比喻义："沐浴在阳光中"。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bathe a wound
- bathe in the sun
- bathe in light

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 清洗伤口
- 沐浴在阳光下
- 沐浴在光线中
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bathe sth in/with sth — 用某物清洗；使某物沐浴在……中
- bathe in sth — 在……中沐浴/沉浸

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bathe sth in/with sth — 用某物清洗；使某物沐浴在……中
- bathe in sth — 在……中沐浴/沉浸
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The nurse **bathed** his wound **with** antiseptic solution. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: The entire valley was **bathed in** golden morning light. (考研英语 2018)
- 介词搭配: She **bathed** her eyes **with** a soothing lotion.
- 介词搭配: The room was **bathed in** soft candlelight.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 护士用消毒液清洗了他的伤口。
- 整个山谷沐浴在金色的晨光之中。
- 她用舒缓的洗液清洗了眼睛。
- 房间里沐浴着柔和的烛光。
</details>

### 23. batter <a id="23-batter"></a>

**Etymology:** Old French *batre* "to beat," from Latin *battuere* "to beat, strike."

**词源：** 古法语 *batre*（"击打"），源自拉丁语 *battuere*（"击打、撞击"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to hit repeatedly and hard
- CN: 连续猛击；殴打

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Implies sustained, heavy beating. Used literally (batter the door down) and metaphorically (battered by criticism). Also used in cooking: battered fish. — 暗示持续的重击；用于字面意义和比喻意义（受到批评的打击）；烹饪中也指裹面糊油炸。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- batter sb/sth
- batter down the door
- battered by storms

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 猛击某人/某物
- 砸破门
- 遭受暴风雨的袭击
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- batter at/on/against sth — 猛击某物
- batter sb into sth — 迫使某人做某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- batter at/on/against sth — 猛击某物
- batter sb into sth — 迫使某人做某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The storm **battered** the coastline, causing widespread damage. (CET-6 2017-06)
- 考研: He was **battered into** submission by his abusive stepfather. (考研英语 2019)
- 介词搭配: The waves **battered against** the rocky shore.
- 介词搭配: The kidnappers **battered** him **into** revealing the safe combination.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 暴风雨猛烈袭击了海岸线，造成了广泛的破坏。
- 他被虐待他的继父打服了。
- 海浪猛烈拍打着岩石海岸。
- 绑匪逼迫他说出保险柜密码。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 24. battle <a id="24-battle"></a>

**Etymology:** Old French *bataille* "battle," from Late Latin *battualia* "practice of fighting," from Latin *battuere* "to beat."

**词源：** 古法语 *bataille*（"战斗"），源自晚期拉丁语 *battualia*（"格斗训练"），源自拉丁语 *battuere*（"击打"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to fight or struggle against
- CN: 战斗；斗争

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- As an intransitive verb: battle with/against. As a transitive verb: battle an illness, battle adversity. More metaphorical than literal in modern usage. — 作不及物动词：battle with/against；作及物动词：battle an illness（与疾病作斗争）、battle adversity（与逆境作斗争）；现代用法中比喻意义多于字面意义。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- battle against/with sb/sth
- battle for sth
- battle an illness/disease

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某人/某物作斗争
- 为某事而战
- 与疾病作斗争
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- battle with/against sb/sth — 与某人/某物斗争
- battle for sth — 为某物而战
- battle over sth — 就某事争执

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- battle with/against sb/sth — 与某人/某物斗争
- battle for sth — 为某物而战
- battle over sth — 就某事争执
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She has been **battling** cancer **for** two years with remarkable courage. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: The two companies are **battling for** market dominance in the tech sector. (考研英语 2021)
- 介词搭配: Firefighters **battled against** the blaze through the night.
- 介词搭配: Lawyers **battled over** the terms of the contract for weeks.
- 介词搭配: The team **battled for** every point in the final match.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她已经以非凡的勇气与癌症抗争了两年。
- 两家公司正在争夺科技领域的市场主导地位。
- 消防员彻夜与大火搏斗。
- 律师们就合同条款争论了数周。
- 这支队伍在决赛中争夺每一分。
</details>

### 25. be <a id="25-be"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bēon* "to be, exist, happen," from Proto-Germanic *\*beuną* "to be, exist," from PIE *\*bʰuH-* "to become, grow."

**词源：** 古英语 *bēon*（"是、存在、发生"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*beuną*（"是、存在"），源自原始印欧语 *\*bʰuH-*（"成为、生长"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to exist; to have a specified quality or identity
- CN: 是；存在；成为

**Part of Speech:** vi./aux.

**Usage Notes:**

- The most fundamental English verb. Functions as a copula (linking subject to complement), an auxiliary (forming passive and continuous tenses), and an existential verb (there be). — 英语中最基本的动词；用作系动词、助动词（构成被动语态和进行时）以及存在动词（there be）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- be about to
- be able to
- be going to
- be + adj.
- there be

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 将要
- 能够
- 将要；打算
- 是……的
- 有（存在）
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be at sth — 在某处
- be in sth — 在……里；处于……状态
- be on sth — 在……上；正在进行

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- be at sth — 在某处
- be in sth — 在……里；处于……状态
- be on sth — 在……上；正在进行
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Academic: There **are** significant differences in gene expression between the two groups. (Nature 2021)
- CET-4/6: The conference **will be** held in Beijing next year. (CET-4 2019-12)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The primary challenge **is** to balance economic growth with environmental protection. (TOEFL TPO 31)
- 介词搭配: She **is at** the library studying for her exams.
- 介词搭配: He **is in** a difficult situation right now.
- 介词搭配: The meeting **is on** Monday afternoon.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 两组之间的基因表达存在显著差异。
- 会议将于明年在北京举行。
- 主要挑战在于平衡经济增长与环境保护。
- 她在图书馆为考试复习。
- 他现在处境困难。
- 会议在周一下午举行。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 26. beam <a id="26-beam"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bēam* "tree, beam, pillar," from Proto-Germanic *\*baumaz* "tree."

**词源：** 古英语 *bēam*（"树、横梁、柱子"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*baumaz*（"树"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to smile radiantly; to emit or transmit light/signals
- CN: 笑容满面；发射（光线、信号）

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- As an intransitive verb, it describes a wide, joyful smile. As a transitive verb, it refers to transmitting signals or light (broadcast, radiate). — 作不及物动词时形容灿烂的喜悦笑容；作及物动词时指发射信号或光线（广播、辐射）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- beam at sb
- beam with joy/pride
- beam a signal/message

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 对某人微笑
- 高兴/自豪地微笑
- 发射信号/发送消息
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- beam at sb — 对某人微笑
- beam with sth — 因某事而面露喜色
- beam sth to sb/sth — 向某人/某物发射某物

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- beam at sb — 对某人微笑
- beam with sth — 因某事而面露喜色
- beam sth to sb/sth — 向某人/某物发射某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The winner **beamed with** pride as she received the gold medal. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: The satellite **beams** data **to** Earth from thousands of miles away. (考研英语 2017)
- 介词搭配: He **beamed at** his daughter as she walked across the stage.
- 介词搭配: The children **beamed with** excitement on Christmas morning.
- 介词搭配: The radio station **beams** its signal **to** listeners across the country.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 获胜者在接受金牌时自豪地微笑着。
- 这颗卫星从数千英里之外向地球传输数据。
- 当女儿走过舞台时，他对她露出了微笑。
- 孩子们在圣诞节早晨兴奋地喜笑颜开。
- 该广播电台向全国听众发射信号。
</details>

### 27. bear <a id="27-bear"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *beran* "to carry, bring; give birth," from Proto-Germanic *\*beraną*, from PIE *\*bʰer-* "to carry."

**词源：** 古英语 *beran*（"携带、带来、生育"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*beraną*，源自原始印欧语 *\*bʰer-*（"携带"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to carry; to endure; to give birth to; to produce
- CN: 承担；忍受；生育；生产

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Highly polysemous. Key meanings: carry (bear a burden), endure (bear the pain), give birth (bear a child), produce (bear fruit). Past participle: born (birth) vs. borne (carried). — 高度多义；主要含义：承担、忍受、生育、生产；过去分词：born（出生）与 borne（承担）有区别。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bear responsibility
- bear the cost
- bear fruit
- bear in mind
- bear with sb

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 承担责任
- 承担费用
- 结出果实；产生效果
- 记住
- 容忍某人；耐心对待某人
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bear on/upon sth — 与某事有关
- bear with sb/sth — 容忍某人/某事
- bear sth out — 证实某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bear on/upon sth — 与某事有关
- bear with sb/sth — 容忍某人/某事
- bear sth out — 证实某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Academic: The data **bears out** the hypothesis that climate change accelerates species migration. (Science 2020)
- CET-4/6: Please **bear with** me while I explain the details of the procedure. (CET-4 2019-06)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The results of the experiment **bear out** our initial predictions. (TOEFL TPO 18)
- 介词搭配: This evidence **bears on** the central question of the trial.
- 介词搭配: If you'll **bear with** me for a moment, I'll find the document.
- 介词搭配: The statistics **bear out** what many have suspected for years.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 数据证实了气候变化加速物种迁徙的假说。
- 请耐心听我解释这个程序的细节。
- 实验结果证实了我们最初的预测。
- 这项证据与审判的核心问题相关。
- 如果你能耐心等我一下，我会找到那份文件。
- 统计数据证实了多年来许多人的猜测。
</details>

### 28. beat <a id="28-beat"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bēatan* "to beat," from Proto-Germanic *\*bautaną* "to push, strike."

**词源：** 古英语 *bēatan*（"击打"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*bautaną*（"推、击"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to strike repeatedly; to defeat; to surpass; to mix vigorously
- CN: 击打；打败；超过；搅拌

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Covers physical striking (beat a drum), competition (beat the opponent), and cooking (beat eggs). Irregular verb: beat → beat → beaten. — 涵盖物理击打、竞赛获胜和烹饪搅拌。不规则动词：beat → beat → beaten。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- beat a record
- beat the competition
- beat eggs
- beat the drum
- beat sb up

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 打破纪录
- 战胜竞争对手
- 打鸡蛋
- 敲鼓
- 痛打某人
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- beat sb at sth — 在某事上打败某人
- beat on/against sth — 拍打某物
- beat sb to sth — 抢先某人做某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- beat sb at sth — 在某事上打败某人
- beat on/against sth — 拍打某物
- beat sb to sth — 抢先某人做某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She **beat** the defending champion **at** the national tennis championship. (CET-4 2019-12)
- 考研: The rain **beat against** the windows throughout the night. (考研英语 2016)
- 介词搭配: He **beat** his opponent **at** chess in three straight games.
- 介词搭配: Waves **beat against** the rocks with tremendous force.
- 介词搭配: I wanted to tell her the news, but someone **beat** me **to** it.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她在全国网球锦标赛中击败了卫冕冠军。
- 雨水整夜敲打着窗户。
- 他在国际象棋中连赢三局击败了对手。
- 海浪以巨大的力量拍打着岩石。
- 我想告诉她这个消息，但有人抢先了一步。
</details>

### 29. beautify <a id="29-beautify"></a>

**Etymology:** From *beauty* + suffix *-fy*, from Latin *-ficare* "to make."

**词源：** 源自 *beauty*（美丽）+ 后缀 *-fy*，源自拉丁语 *-ficare*（"使……"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to make beautiful or more beautiful
- CN: 美化；使美丽

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Always transitive. Often used in urban planning, gardening, and personal grooming contexts. Less common in everyday speech; "make beautiful" is sometimes preferred. — 总是及物动词；常用于城市规划、园艺和个人美容；日常口语中不如 "make beautiful" 常用。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- beautify the city
- beautify a garden
- beautify oneself

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 美化城市
- 美化花园
- 打扮自己
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- beautify sth with sth — 用某物美化某物

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- beautify sth with sth — 用某物美化某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The local government launched a project to **beautify** the downtown area. (CET-4 2017-06)
- 考研: They **beautified** the campus **with** new flower beds and sculptures. (考研英语 2018)
- 介词搭配: The community volunteers **beautified** the park **with** fresh paint and new benches.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 当地政府启动了一个美化市中心区的项目。
- 他们用新的花坛和雕塑美化了校园。
- 社区志愿者用新油漆和新长椅美化了公园。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 30. beckon <a id="30-beckon"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bīecnan* "to make a sign," from Proto-Germanic *\*bauknjaną* "to signal."

**词源：** 古英语 *bīecnan*（"做手势"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*bauknjaną*（"发出信号"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to gesture or signal to someone to come; to attract or lure
- CN: 招手示意；吸引；召唤

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be used both literally (beckon someone over) and figuratively (adventure beckons). — 既可用作字面意义（招手让人过来），也可用作比喻意义（冒险在召唤）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- beckon sb over
- beckon to sb
- beckon sb forward

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 招手让某人过来
- 向某人示意
- 示意某人前进
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- beckon to sb — 向某人招手示意
- beckon sb into/to sth — 招手示意某人进入/去某处

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- beckon to sb — 向某人招手示意
- beckon sb into/to sth — 招手示意某人进入/去某处
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The waiter **beckoned** **to** us from across the restaurant. (CET-4 2016-12)
- 考研: The promise of a better life **beckoned** her **to** the big city. (考研英语 2020)
- 介词搭配: She **beckoned to** the child to come closer.
- 介词搭配: The exotic beaches **beckoned** tourists **to** the island year after year.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 服务员从餐厅的另一头向我们招手示意。
- 更好生活的承诺召唤她去了大城市。
- 她向孩子招手，示意他再靠近一些。
- 充满异域风情的海滩年复一年地吸引着游客前往该岛。
</details>

### 31. become <a id="31-become"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *becuman* "to come, happen, befall," from *be-* "to be" + *cuman* "to come."

**词源：** 古英语 *becuman*（"来到、发生、降临"），源自 *be-*（"是"）+ *cuman*（"来"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to begin to be; to develop into; to suit or be appropriate for
- CN: 成为；变得；适合

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a copula + adjective/comparative (become rich, become more important). As a transitive verb (formal): "That dress becomes you" (suits you). — 作系动词 + 形容词/比较级（变得富有、变得更重要）；作及物动词（正式用法）："That dress becomes you"（那件连衣裙很适合你）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- become + adj.
- become of sb/sth
- become a habit

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 变得……
- 某人/某物怎么样了
- 成为习惯
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- become of sb/sth — 某人/某物发生/遭遇（常与 what/whoever 连用）

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- become of sb/sth — 某人/某物发生/遭遇（常与 what/whoever 连用）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Over the years, her hobby **became** a successful business. (CET-4 2018-12)
- TOEFL/IELTS: As the climate warms, many species **become** more vulnerable to disease. (TOEFL TPO 25)
- 介词搭配: Whatever **became of** that old friend you used to travel with?
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 几年来，她的爱好成了一项成功的事业。
- 随着气候变暖，许多物种变得更容易受到疾病侵袭。
- 你以前一起旅行的老朋友后来怎么样了？
</details>

### 32. bed <a id="32-bed"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bedd* "bed, couch," from Proto-Germanic *\*badją* "sleeping place."

**词源：** 古英语 *bedd*（"床、卧榻"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*badją*（"睡觉的地方"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to provide with a place to sleep; to embed or fix in a foundation
- CN: 为……提供床铺；嵌入；固定

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- When used as a verb, "bed" often appears in construction (bed a pipe in concrete) or gardening (bed out plants). "Bed down" means to settle into a place to sleep. — 作动词时常用于建筑（用混凝土固定管道）或园艺（栽种植物）；"bed down" 意为安顿下来睡觉。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bed down
- bed sb down
- bed plants out

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 安顿睡觉
- 给某人安排床铺
- 把植物移栽到地里
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bed down in/on sth — 在某处睡觉/安顿
- bed sth in sth — 将某物嵌入某物

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bed down in/on sth — 在某处睡觉/安顿
- bed sth in sth — 将某物嵌入某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Common: The soldiers **bedded down** **in** an abandoned barn for the night. (Common)
- CET-4/6: The engineer **bedded** the new foundation **in** concrete to ensure stability. (CET-6 2017-12)
- 介词搭配: We **bedded down on** the floor because there were not enough beds.
- 介词搭配: The pipe was **bedded in** a layer of gravel.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 士兵们在一个废弃的谷仓里安顿下来过夜。
- 工程师将新地基嵌入混凝土以确保稳定。
- 因为没有足够的床，我们就在地板上打地铺。
- 管道被铺设在碎石层中。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 33. beg <a id="33-beg"></a>

**Etymology:** Middle English *beggen*, perhaps from Old English *bedecian* "to beg," related to *bed* "prayer."

**词源：** 中古英语 *beggen*，可能源自古英语 *bedecian*（"乞讨"），与 *bed*（"祈祷"）相关。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to ask for earnestly or humbly; to ask for money/food as charity
- CN: 恳求；乞讨

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: beg for sth. Transitive: beg sb to do sth. "Beg the question" is a fixed logical fallacy phrase (often misused to mean "raise the question"). — 不及物：beg for sth（乞求某物）；及物：beg sb to do sth（恳求某人做某事）；"beg the question" 是逻辑谬误的固定短语（常被误用为"引发问题"）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- beg for sth
- beg sb to do sth
- beg forgiveness
- beg the question

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 乞求某物
- 恳求某人做某事
- 乞求原谅
- 回避问题；让人质疑
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- beg for sth — 乞求某物
- beg of sb — 请求某人

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- beg for sth — 乞求某物
- beg of sb — 请求某人
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The children **begged** their parents **for** a puppy for months. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: I **beg** **of** you to reconsider your decision before it's too late. (考研英语 2015)
- 介词搭配: She **begged for** another chance to prove herself.
- 介词搭配: He **begged of** her to stay just a little longer.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 孩子们连续几个月恳求父母买一只小狗。
- 我恳求你在为时已晚之前重新考虑你的决定。
- 她恳求再给她一次证明自己的机会。
- 他恳求她再多待一会儿。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 34. begin <a id="34-begin"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *beginnan* "to begin," from Proto-Germanic *\*biginnaną*, from *bi-* "be-" + *\*ginnaną* "to open, cut open."

**词源：** 古英语 *beginnan*（"开始"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*biginnaną*，源自 *bi-*（"be-"）+ *\*ginnaną*（"打开、切开"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to start; to commence
- CN: 开始

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be followed by both infinitive (begin to do) and gerund (begin doing) with little difference in meaning. "To begin with" is an adverb meaning "firstly." — 后接不定式和动名词均可，意义差别不大；"to begin with" 是副词短语，意为"首先"。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- begin to do sth
- begin doing sth
- begin with sth
- to begin with

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 开始做某事
- 开始做某事
- 从某事开始
- 首先；一开始
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- begin with sth — 从某事开始
- begin by doing sth — 以做某事开始

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- begin with sth — 从某事开始
- begin by doing sth — 以做某事开始
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The ceremony will **begin with** a speech from the president. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 考研: Let me **begin by** thanking everyone for their support. (考研英语 2018)
- 介词搭配: The concert **began with** a beautiful piano piece.
- 介词搭配: She **began by** outlining the main objectives of the project.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 典礼将以校长的致辞开始。
- 首先，我要感谢大家的支持。
- 音乐会以一首优美的钢琴曲开始。
- 她首先概述了该项目的主要目标。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 35. behave <a id="35-behave"></a>

**Etymology:** From *be-* + *have*, modeled on Middle English *behaven*. Originally "to have or hold oneself in a certain way."

**词源：** 源自 *be-* + *have*，仿照中古英语 *behaven*；原意为"以某种方式表现自己"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to act or conduct oneself in a specified way, especially appropriately
- CN: 行为；举止；守规矩

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: behave well/badly. Transitive (reflexive): behave oneself (better behaved). Most common in parenting and classroom settings. — 不及物：behave well/badly；及物（反身）：behave oneself（表现好）；最常用于亲子教育和课堂管理。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- behave well/badly
- behave oneself
- behave like sb/sth

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 表现好/坏
- 守规矩
- 表现得像某人/某物
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- behave towards/to sb — 对待某人（的方式）
- behave like sb/sth — 表现得像某人/某物

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- behave towards/to sb — 对待某人（的方式）
- behave like sb/sth — 表现得像某人/某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The children were told to **behave themselves** at the museum. (CET-4 2017-06)
- 考研: In the experiment, participants **behaved** differently **towards** those they believed to be authorities. (考研英语 2019)
- 介词搭配: She **behaved towards** her colleagues with respect and courtesy.
- 介词搭配: He **behaved like** a child when he didn't get his way.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 孩子们被告知在博物馆里要守规矩。
- 在实验中，参与者对待他们认为有权威的人的方式有所不同。
- 她对待同事充满尊重和礼貌。
- 当他不如意时，表现得像个孩子。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 36. believe <a id="36-believe"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *belȳfan* "to believe," from Proto-Germanic *\*galaubijaną* "to hold dear, be persuaded."

**词源：** 古英语 *belȳfan*（"相信"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*galaubijaną*（"珍视、被说服"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to accept as true; to have faith or confidence
- CN: 相信；认为

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: believe sth/sb, believe that... Intransitive: believe in (have faith in the existence/effectiveness of). "Believe so" vs. "believe not" for short answers. — 及物：believe sth/sb、believe that...；不及物：believe in（相信存在/有效）；简略回答用 believe so / believe not。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- believe in sb/sth
- believe it or not
- believe so
- make believe

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 相信某人/某物的存在；信任
- 信不信由你
- 认为是这样
- 假装
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- believe in sb/sth — 相信某人/某事；信任

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- believe in sb/sth — 相信某人/某事；信任
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I **believe** **in** the power of education to change lives. (CET-4 2019-12)
- Academic: Most researchers now **believe** that the extinction event was caused by an asteroid impact. (Nature 2019)
- 介词搭配: Do you **believe in** ghosts?
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我相信教育改变人生的力量。
- 如今大多数研究人员认为那次灭绝事件是由小行星撞击造成的。
- 你相信有鬼吗？
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 37. belong <a id="37-belong"></a>

**Etymology:** Middle English *belongen*, from *be-* + *longen* "to be appropriate," from Old English *langian* "to pertain."

**词源：** 中古英语 *belongen*，源自 *be-* + *longen*（"合适"），源自 *langian*（"属于"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be in the right place; to be owned by; to be a member of
- CN: 属于；应被放置；是……的成员

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Always intransitive and always requires a prepositional phrase or adverb. "Belong to" for ownership/membership; "belong in/on/under" for appropriate location. — 总是不及物，且必须接介词短语或副词；"belong to" 表示所有权/成员身份；"belong in/on/under" 表示合适的位置。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- belong to sb/sth
- belong in/on/under
- belong together

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 属于某人/某物
- 应该放在……里/上/下
- 属于彼此；很般配
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- belong to sb/sth — 属于某人/某物
- belong in/on/under sth — 应放在某处

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- belong to sb/sth — 属于某人/某物
- belong in/on/under sth — 应放在某处
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: This book **belongs to** the library, so please return it on time. (CET-4 2016-06)
- 考研: As a scholar, he **belongs in** a university, not a corporate office. (考研英语 2018)
- 介词搭配: The painting **belongs to** a private collector.
- 介词搭配: Those dishes **belong in** the kitchen cabinet.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这本书属于图书馆，请按时归还。
- 作为一名学者，他属于大学，而不是公司办公室。
- 这幅画属于一位私人收藏家。
- 那些盘子应该放在厨房的碗柜里。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 38. bend <a id="38-bend"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *bendan* "to bend a bow, fetter," from Proto-Germanic *\*bandijaną* "to bind, fetter."

**词源：** 古英语 *bendan*（"拉弓、束缚"），源自原始日耳曼语 *\*bandijaną*（"绑、束缚"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to curve or force out of straight shape; to lean; to submit
- CN: 弯曲；弯腰；屈服

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: bend a wire. Intransitive: the road bends, bend over/down. Figurative: bend the rules (interpret loosely), bend to sb's will. — 及物：弯曲铁丝；不及物：路转弯、弯腰；比喻：bend the rules（变通规则）、bend to sb's will（屈从于某人意志）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bend down/over/forward
- bend the rules
- bend sb's ear
- bend to pressure

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 弯腰
- 变通规则
- 缠着某人说话
- 在压力下屈服
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bend over sth — 弯腰于某物之上
- bend to sth — 屈服于某事
- bend sth into sth — 把某物弯成某形状

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bend over sth — 弯腰于某物之上
- bend to sth — 屈服于某事
- bend sth into sth — 把某物弯成某形状
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He **bent** **over** the table to pick up the fallen pen. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: The manager refused to **bend** **to** the demands of the protesters. (考研英语 2017)
- 介词搭配: She **bent over** the baby's crib to tuck him in.
- 介词搭配: The government eventually **bent to** public pressure.
- 介词搭配: He **bent** the wire **into** a perfect circle.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他弯腰到桌子下面捡起了掉落的钢笔。
- 经理拒绝屈从于抗议者的要求。
- 她俯身在婴儿床上帮他盖好被子。
- 政府最终在公众压力下屈服了。
- 他把铁丝弯成了一个完美的圆形。
</details>

### 39. benefit <a id="39-benefit"></a>

**Etymology:** Anglo-Norman *benfet* "good action," from Latin *benefactum* "good deed," from *bene* "well" + *facere* "to do."

**词源：** 盎格鲁-诺曼语 *benfet*（"善行"），源自拉丁语 *benefactum*（"好事"），源自 *bene*（"好"）+ *facere*（"做"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to gain an advantage; to be useful to
- CN: 受益；有益于

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: benefit from/by. Transitive: benefit sb/sth. The noun form is far more common; the verb is slightly formal. — 不及物：benefit from/by（从……受益）；及物：benefit sb/sth（对……有益）；名词形式更常见，动词略显正式。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- benefit from sth
- benefit greatly/enormously
- mutual benefit

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 从某事中受益
- 大大受益
- 互利
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- benefit from/by sth — 从某事中受益

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- benefit from/by sth — 从某事中受益
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Academic: Patients **benefited from** the new treatment, with a 40% reduction in symptoms. (The Lancet 2020)
- CET-4/6: Small businesses can **benefit from** the new tax cuts. (CET-4 2019-06)
- 介词搭配: Students **benefited greatly from** the scholarship program.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 患者从新疗法中受益，症状减少了40%。
- 小企业可以从新的减税政策中受益。
- 学生们从奖学金项目中受益匪浅。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 40. beset <a id="40-beset"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *besettan* "to surround, besiege," from *be-* "around" + *settlan* "to set, place."

**词源：** 古英语 *besettan*（"包围、围攻"），源自 *be-*（"在周围"）+ *settlan*（"放置"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to trouble or threaten persistently; to surround
- CN: 困扰；围困

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Almost always used in the passive voice (beset by/with). Strongly implies continuous or repeated trouble. Formal/literary register. — 几乎总用于被动语态（beset by/with）；强烈暗示持续或反复的困扰；正式/文学语体。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- beset by problems/difficulties
- beset with doubts
- beset on all sides

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 被问题/困难所困扰
- 充满疑虑
- 四面楚歌
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- beset by/with sth — 被某事困扰；充满某物

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- beset by/with sth — 被某事困扰；充满某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The project was **beset by** technical difficulties from the very beginning. (CET-6 2017-12)
- Academic: The region remains **beset by** conflict and economic instability. (Nature 2020)
- 介词搭配: The expedition was **beset with** challenges at every stage.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 这个项目从一开始就受到技术难题的困扰。
- 该地区仍然饱受冲突和经济不稳定之苦。
- 这次探险在每一个阶段都面临着重重挑战。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 41. besiege <a id="41-besiege"></a>

**Etymology:** Middle English *besegen*, from *be-* "around" + *segen* "to lay siege to," from Old French *sege* "seat, siege."

**词源：** 中古英语 *besegen*，源自 *be-*（"在周围"）+ *segen*（"围攻"），源自古法语 *sege*（"座位、围攻"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to surround with armed forces; to overwhelm with requests or questions
- CN: 围攻；围困；被（请求、问题）淹没

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Literally: military siege (besiege a city). Figuratively: besieged by fans/reporters. Stronger than "beset" — implies active, aggressive surrounding. — 字面意义：军事围攻（围攻城市）；比喻意义：被粉丝/记者包围；比 "beset" 语气更强，暗示主动的、攻击性的包围。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- besiege a city/fortress
- besiege sb with questions
- besieged by fans

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 围攻城市/堡垒
- 用问题纠缠某人
- 被粉丝包围
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- besiege sb with sth — 用某物（问题/请求等）纠缠某人
- be besieged by sb — 被某人包围/围困

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- besiege sb with sth — 用某物（问题/请求等）纠缠某人
- be besieged by sb — 被某人包围/围困
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The celebrity was **besieged by** fans asking for autographs. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: The committee was **besieged with** complaints about the new policy. (考研英语 2019)
- 介词搭配: The mayor was **besieged by** journalists after the scandal broke.
- 介词搭配: The help desk was **besieged with** calls all morning.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 那位名人被索要签名的粉丝团团围住。
- 委员会收到了大量关于新政策的投诉。
- 丑闻曝光后，市长被记者们围堵。
- 整个上午求助热线电话不断。
</details>

### 42. bet <a id="42-bet"></a>

**Etymology:** 16th century, probably from *abet* "to incite, encourage," or from the noun *bet* "a wager," of uncertain origin.

**词源：** 16世纪，可能源自 *abet*（"煽动、鼓励"），或源自名词 *bet*（"赌注"），来源不确定。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to risk money on an outcome; to be certain
- CN: 打赌；敢肯定

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Irregular verb: bet → bet → bet. Intransitive: bet on sth. Transitive: bet money, bet sb sth. "I bet" is an informal way to say "I'm sure." — 不规则动词：bet → bet → bet；不及物：bet on sth；及物：赌钱、与某人打赌某物；"I bet" 是口语中表示"我敢肯定"的方式。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bet on sth
- bet sb sth
- you bet
- I bet

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 在某事上下注
- 与某人打赌某物
- 当然；没问题
- 我敢肯定；我打赌
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bet on sth — 在某事上下注
- bet against sb/sth — 赌某人/某事不会成功

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bet on sth — 在某事上下注
- bet against sb/sth — 赌某人/某事不会成功
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I **bet** you 20 dollars that our team will win the match. (CET-4 2016-06)
- 考研: Many investors **bet against** the struggling company's recovery. (考研英语 2020)
- 介词搭配: He **bet** all his savings **on** the outcome of the race.
- 介词搭配: It's risky to **bet against** a company with such strong fundamentals.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 我跟你赌20美元，我们队会赢。
- 许多投资者打赌这家陷入困境的公司不会复苏。
- 他把所有积蓄都押在了比赛结果上。
- 与基本面如此强劲的公司对赌是有风险的。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 43. betray <a id="43-betray"></a>

**Etymology:** Middle English *betraien*, from *be-* "thoroughly" + Old French *trair* "to betray," from Latin *tradere* "to hand over."

**词源：** 中古英语 *betraien*，源自 *be-*（"彻底"）+ 古法语 *trair*（"背叛"），源自拉丁语 *tradere*（"交出"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be disloyal to; to reveal unintentionally
- CN: 背叛；泄露（秘密）；暴露（情感）

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Core meaning: violate trust (betray a friend). Extended meaning: unintentionally reveal (betray one's emotions). The noun form "betrayal" is very common. — 核心意义：辜负信任（背叛朋友）；引申意义：无意中暴露（出卖情感）；名词 "betrayal" 非常常见。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- betray sb's trust
- betray a secret
- betray one's feelings/emotions
- feel betrayed

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 辜负某人的信任
- 泄露秘密
- 暴露某人的情感
- 感到被背叛
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- betray sb to sb — 向某人出卖某人
- betray sth to sb — 向某人泄露某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- betray sb to sb — 向某人出卖某人
- betray sth to sb — 向某人泄露某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He felt that his closest colleague had **betrayed** his trust. (CET-6 2018-12)
- 考研: Despite her calm exterior, her trembling hands **betrayed** her nervousness. (考研英语 2017)
- 介词搭配: The informant **betrayed** the spy ring **to** the authorities.
- 介词搭配: She refused to **betray** the location of the hideout **to** anyone.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 他觉得最亲密的同事辜负了他的信任。
- 尽管她外表平静，但颤抖的双手暴露了她的紧张。
- 线人向当局出卖了整个间谍网。
- 她拒绝向任何人泄露藏身之处的位置。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 44. better <a id="44-better"></a>

**Etymology:** Old English *beterian* "to make better, improve," from *betera* "better."

**词源：** 古英语 *beterian*（"改善"），源自 *betera*（"更好的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to improve upon; to surpass
- CN: 改善；超过

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- As a verb, less common than the adjective/adverb form. Often used in self-improvement contexts (better oneself) and competitive contexts (better a record). — 作动词比作形容词/副词形式少见；常用于自我提升语境（better oneself）和竞争语境（打破纪录）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- better oneself
- better the situation
- better a record

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 提升自己；改善自己
- 改善情况
- 打破纪录
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- better sb/sth in sth — 在某方面超过某人/某物

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- better sb/sth in sth — 在某方面超过某人/某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She took evening classes to **better** herself professionally. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: The new policy aims to **better** the living conditions of migrant workers. (考研英语 2019)
- 介词搭配: The latest model **betters** its predecessor **in** both speed and efficiency.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她上夜校以提升自己的专业水平。
- 新政策旨在改善农民工的生活条件。
- 最新型号在速度和效率上都超过了前代产品。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 45. beware <a id="45-beware"></a>

**Etymology:** From *be* + *ware* "aware, cautious," from Old English *wær* "cautious."

**词源：** 源自 *be* + *ware*（"警觉、谨慎"），源自古英语 *wær*（"谨慎的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to be cautious or wary of
- CN: 当心；提防

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Almost exclusively used in the imperative or infinitive. No conjugation (*bewares not used). "Beware of" is standard; "beware" followed by a noun directly is possible in warnings (Beware the dog). — 几乎只用于祈使句或不定式；没有动词变位（*bewares 不成立）；标准用法是 "beware of"；在警告语中可直接接名词（Beware the dog）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- beware of sth/sb
- beware of doing sth
- beware the ...

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 当心某物/某人
- 注意不要做某事
- 当心……
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- beware of sth/sb — 当心某物/某人
- beware of doing sth — 注意不要做某事

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- beware of sth/sb — 当心某物/某人
- beware of doing sth — 注意不要做某事
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: **Beware of** pickpockets when you travel in crowded tourist areas. (CET-4 2018-06)
- 考研: Investors should **beware of** putting all their money into a single stock. (考研英语 2020)
- 介词搭配: **Beware of** the dog — it can be aggressive.
- 介词搭配: **Beware of** signing any contract without reading it thoroughly.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 在拥挤的旅游区旅行时要当心扒手。
- 投资者应注意不要把所有钱都投到一只股票上。
- 当心那条狗——它可能会咬人。
- 注意不要在未仔细阅读的情况下签署任何合同。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 46. bewilder <a id="46-bewilder"></a>

**Etymology:** From *be-* + *wildern* "to go astray," from *wild* "wild."

**词源：** 源自 *be-* + *wildern*（"迷路"），源自 *wild*（"野外的"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to perplex or confuse greatly
- CN: 使迷惑；使困惑

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Stronger than "confuse" — implies complete disorientation. Usually used in passive voice: be bewildered by/at. — 比 "confuse" 程度更强，表示完全不知所措；通常用于被动语态：be bewildered by/at。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bewilder sb
- be bewildered by/at sth
- completely/totally bewildered

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人困惑
- 被某事弄糊涂
- 完全困惑不解
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- be bewildered by/at sth — 被某事弄糊涂

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- be bewildered by/at sth — 被某事弄糊涂
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The complexity of the new tax laws **bewildered** many citizens. (CET-6 2017-06)
- 考研: She was **bewildered by** the sudden change in her friend's attitude. (考研英语 2018)
- 介词搭配: The tourists were **bewildered by** the confusing street signs.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 新税法的复杂性让许多市民困惑不已。
- 她被朋友态度的突然变化弄糊涂了。
- 游客们被混乱的路牌弄得晕头转向。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 47. bias <a id="47-bias"></a>

**Etymology:** From French *biais* "a slant, a slope," of uncertain origin.

**词源：** 源自法语 *biais*（"倾斜"），来源不确定。

**Definitions:**

- EN: to influence unfairly; to cause prejudice in
- CN: 使存偏见；影响（偏向）

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Most commonly used in the passive or past participle form: biased. The noun form is more common. In statistics/research contexts: bias the results, selection bias. — 最常用作被动语态或过去分词形式（biased）；名词形式更常见；在统计/研究语境中：bias the results（使结果产生偏差）、selection bias（选择偏差）。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bias sb/sth
- bias the results
- bias against/toward
- be biased

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 使某人/某物产生偏见
- 使结果产生偏差
- 对……有偏见/偏向
- 有偏见的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bias sb against/toward sb/sth — 使某人对某人/某物有偏见/偏向

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- bias sb against/toward sb/sth — 使某人对某人/某物有偏见/偏向
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- Academic: The study design was carefully controlled to avoid **biasing** the results. (Nature 2021)
- CET-4/6: Media coverage can **bias** public opinion **against** minority groups. (CET-6 2018-06)
- 介词搭配: Prior experience can **bias** a person **toward** a particular solution.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 研究设计经过精心控制，以避免结果出现偏差。
- 媒体报道可能会使公众舆论对少数群体产生偏见。
- 先前的经验可能会使一个人倾向于某种特定的解决方案。
</details>

[↑ Back to Top](#top)

### 48. bid <a id="48-bid"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _biddan_ "to ask, entreat, pray," also _beodan_ "to offer, command," from Proto-Germanic _bidjaną_ "to ask."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _biddan_ "请求、恳求、祈祷"和 _beodan_ "提供、命令"，来自原始日耳曼语 _bidjaną_ "请求"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To offer a price for something, especially at an auction; to command or order
- CN: 出价；命令，吩咐

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bid" can be both regular (past tense "bidded" only in specific card game contexts) and irregular (bid -> bid -> bid); the irregular form is standard for auctions and commands. — "Bid" 的过去式在拍卖和命令语境中为不规则变化 bid-bid-bid，仅在纸牌游戏中用 bidded。
- When used to mean "command," it is somewhat archaic and formal, often found in literary or ceremonial language. — 作"命令"解时较为古雅正式，常见于文学或仪式用语。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bid on a project
- bid for a contract
- bid farewell
- bid someone welcome
- bid defiance
- bid adieu
- highest bidder
- bid against someone
- bid up the price
- bid aggressively
- successful bid
- bid on eBay
- competitive bidding

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 投标一个项目
- 竞标合同
- 告别
- 欢迎某人
- 公然违抗
- 告别
- 最高出价人
- 与某人竞拍
- 抬高价格
- 积极出价
- 成功中标
- 在eBay上出价
- 竞争性投标
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bid on + item (auction)
- bid for + contract/project
- bid against + competitor
- bid up + price

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对某物出价（拍卖）
- 竞标合同/项目
- 与竞争对手竞拍
- 抬高价格
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The company decided to bid on the construction project. (CET-6 2018-06)
- 考研: Several firms are bidding for the lucrative contract. (考研英语 2015)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The antique vase attracted bidders from around the world. (TOEFL TPO 34)
- Academic: Bidding strategies in online auctions have been widely studied. (Nature/2020)
- Common: We went to the auction to bid on a painting.
- 介词搭配: She bid against three other collectors for the rare stamp.
- 介词搭配: Speculators tried to bid up the stock price before the announcement.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 该公司决定对这个建设项目投标。
- 几家公司在竞标这份利润丰厚的合同。
- 那个古董花瓶吸引了来自世界各地的竞拍者。
- 在线拍卖中的出价策略已被广泛研究。
- 我们去拍卖会竞拍一幅画。
- 她与另外三位收藏家竞拍这枚珍稀邮票。
- 投机者在公告前试图推高股价。
</details>
