### 49. bill <a id="49-bill"></a>

**Etymology:** From Anglo-French _bille_ "list, account," from Medieval Latin _billa_ "document," from Latin _bulla_ "sealed document."

**词源：** 来自盎格鲁-法语 _bille_ "清单、账目"，源自中世纪拉丁语 _billa_ "文件"，拉丁语 _bulla_ "密封文件"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To send someone an invoice; to charge; to advertise or publicize
- CN: 给某人开账单；收费；宣传，推销

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bill" as a verb often appears in passive constructions: "I was billed for the service." — 作为动词常出现在被动结构中。
- In informal American English, "bill" can mean to advertise a product or service aggressively. — 在非正式美式英语中，可表示大力宣传产品或服务。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bill someone for
- bill at a rate
- bill monthly
- bill the client
- bill the customer
- billable hours
- over-bill
- bill a service
- bill as a cost
- bill separately
- bill in arrears

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 向某人开账单
- 按费率收费
- 按月计费
- 向客户收费
- 向顾客收费
- 可计费小时数
- 多收费
- 对服务收费
- 计作成本
- 单独计费
- 事后付款
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bill for + service/item
- bill at + price/rate
- bill as + role/description

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为服务/项目开账单
- 按价格/费率收费
- 宣传为某角色/描述
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The lawyer billed the client for 50 hours of work. (CET-6 2017-12)
- 考研: The repair shop billed me for parts I never ordered. (考研英语 2014)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Hotels typically bill guests at checkout. (TOEFL TPO 28)
- Academic: Telemedicine consultations are billed at a lower rate. (The Lancet/2021)
- Common: They billed the event as the biggest show of the year.
- 介词搭配: The plumber billed me for the emergency call-out.
- 介词搭配: The concert was billed as the farewell tour.
- 介词搭配: The service is billed at $20 per hour.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 律师向客户收取了50小时的工时费。
- 修理店对我从未订购的零件收了费。
- 酒店通常在退房时向客人收费。
- 远程医疗咨询按较低费率收费。
- 他们把这个活动宣传为年度最大演出。
- 水管工向我收取了紧急上门的费用。
- 这场音乐会被称为告别巡演。
- 该服务按每小时20美元收费。
</details>

### 50. bin <a id="50-bin"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _binne_ "basket, manger," from Celtic origin, reinforced by French _benne_ "basket."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _binne_ "篮子、食槽"，源自凯尔特语，后受法语 _benne_ "篮子"加强。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To discard or throw something away; to reject or get rid of
- CN: 扔掉，丢弃；拒绝，抛弃

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bin" is primarily British English; American English equivalent is "trash" or "throw away." — 主要用于英式英语，美式英语对应词为 trash 或 throw away。
- Increasingly used in computing contexts ("bin an email" or "bin a file"). — 在计算机语境中日益常见。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bin the rubbish
- bin unwanted items
- bin the idea
- bin the whole thing
- bin the document
- bin the draft
- bin the plan
- bin immediately
- bin without hesitation
- bin the application
- bin the evidence

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 扔掉垃圾
- 丢弃不需要的物品
- 放弃这个想法
- 放弃整件事
- 丢弃文件
- 丢弃草稿
- 放弃计划
- 立即丢弃
- 毫不犹豫地丢弃
- 拒绝申请
- 销毁证据
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bin in + container
- bin off + plan/event (UK slang)

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 扔进某容器
- 取消计划/活动（英式俚语）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: I decided to bin the old newspapers. (CET-4 2016-12)
- 考研: The committee binned the proposal after the review. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The editor binned the entire draft and started over. (TOEFL TPO 42)
- Academic: Participants were instructed to bin any contaminated samples. (Nature/2019)
- Common: Just bin that junk mail.
- 介词搭配: He binned the receipt in the trash can.
- 介词搭配: She binned off the party at the last minute.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

### 51. bind <a id="51-bind"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _bindan_ "to tie up, fetter," from Proto-Germanic _bindanan_, from PIE root _bhendh-_ "to bind."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _bindan_ "捆绑、束缚"，来自原始日耳曼语 _bindanan_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhendh-_ "绑"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To tie or fasten something tightly; to unite or obligate; to impose a legal or moral duty
- CN: 捆绑，系紧；结合；使承担法律或道德义务

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bind" can be either transitive or intransitive: "This glue binds strongly" (intransitive) vs. "Bind the papers together" (transitive). — 既可作及物也可作不及物动词。
- In passive voice, often used to describe contractual or moral obligations: "I am bound by the agreement." — 被动语态常用于描述合同或道德义务。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bind together
- bind up a wound
- bind the contract
- bind hands
- bind tightly
- bind with rope
- bind by law
- bind in solidarity
- bind the pages
- bind fast
- bind securely
- bind the agreement
- legally binding

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 绑在一起
- 包扎伤口
- 约束合同
- 绑住双手
- 紧紧捆绑
- 用绳子捆绑
- 受法律约束
- 团结一致
- 装订书页
- 绑牢
- 安全绑定
- 约束协议
- 具有法律约束力
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bind with + material/tool
- bind to + object/person
- bind by + rule/agreement
- bind in + volume (publishing)

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用某材料/工具捆绑
- 绑定到某物/某人
- 受规则/协议约束
- 装订成册（出版）
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The nurse bound the wound with a clean bandage. (CET-4 2017-06)

- 介词搭配: She bound the package with strong tape.
- 介词搭配: The contract binds you to the company for two years.
- 介词搭配: We are bound by the rules of the competition.
- 介词搭配: The book was bound in fine Moroccan leather.
</details>

<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她用结实的胶带把包裹捆好。

- 我们受比赛规则的约束。
- 这本书用精美的摩洛哥皮革装订。
</details>

### 52. bite <a id="52-bite"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _bitan_ "to cut into with teeth," from Proto-Germanic _beitanan_, from PIE root _bheid-_ "to split."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _bitan_ "用牙齿切入"，来自原始日耳曼语 _beitanan_，源自原始印欧语根 _bheid-_ "劈开"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To cut or pierce with the teeth; to sting (insect); to take the bait
- CN: 咬；叮，蜇；上钩

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Irregular verb: bite -> bit -> bitten. — 不规则动词：bite -> bit -> bitten。
- Often used figuratively: "bite the bullet" (face something difficult), "bite off more than one can chew" (take on too much). — 常用于比喻义。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bite into
- bite off
- bite down
- bite hard
- bite the dust
- bite the bullet
- bite one's tongue
- bite one's lips
- bite the hand that feeds
- bite the apple
- bite back
- bite deeply
- bite-sized

- 一口大小的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bite into + object
- bite off + piece

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

### 53. blackmail <a id="53-blackmail"></a>

**Etymology:** From _black_ + obsolete Scottish _mail_ "rent, tribute," from Old Norse _mal_ "agreement, speech."

**词源：** 来自 _black_（黑色）+ 已废弃的苏格兰语 _mail_ "租金、贡品"，源自古诺尔斯语 _mal_ "协议、话语"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To demand money or favors from someone by threatening to reveal compromising information
- CN: 敲诈，勒索

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- "Blackmail" is always transitive; you blackmail someone into doing something. — 始终是及物动词：blackmail someone into doing something。
- The noun form is far more common, but the verb is widely recognized and used. — 名词形式更常见，但动词形式也被广泛使用。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blackmail someone into
- blackmail for money
- attempt to blackmail
- try to blackmail
- blackmail a person
- blackmail a company
- blackmail with threats
- blackmail using evidence
- blackmail over an affair
- emotional blackmail
- blackmail letter
- blackmail scheme

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 勒索某人做某事
- 勒索钱财
- 企图勒索
- 试图勒索
- 勒索某人
- 敲诈公司
- 以威胁勒索
- 利用证据勒索
- 因婚外情勒索
- 情感勒索
- 敲诈信
- 勒索计划
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blackmail into + doing something
- blackmail for + money/favors
- blackmail over + subject

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 勒索某人做某事
- 为钱财/好处而勒索
- 因某事而勒索
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The criminal tried to blackmail the celebrity into paying him. (CET-6 2018-12)

- TOEFL/IELTS: The detective uncovered a plot to blackmail the senator. (TOEFL TPO 38)

- 介词搭配: They blackmailed the politician into resigning.
- 介词搭配: He blackmailed her for thousands of dollars.
- 介词搭配: The scandal involved blackmailing the CEO over his business dealings.
</details>

### 54. blame <a id="54-blame"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French _blasmer_ "to reprove, condemn," from Latin _blasphemare_ "to blaspheme," from Greek _blasphemein_ "to speak evil of."

**词源：** 源自古法语 _blasmer_ "责备、谴责"，源自拉丁语 _blasphemare_ "亵渎"，源自希腊语 _blasphemein_ "说坏话"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To hold someone responsible for a fault or wrongdoing; to assign responsibility for something bad
- CN: 责备，归咎于

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Common construction: "blame A for B" or "blame B on A." — 常用结构：blame A for B 或 blame B on A。
- Passive form "A is to blame" is idiomatic and means "A is responsible." — 被动形式 A is to blame 是惯用法，表示"该怪A"。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blame for
- blame on
- blame unfairly
- blame entirely
- blame partly
- blame the victim
- blame oneself
- blame the system
- blame the weather
- take the blame
- shift the blame
- assign blame

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 因某事责备
- 归咎于某事
- 不公正地责备
- 完全归咎于
- 部分归咎于
- 责怪受害者
- 自责
- 归咎于体制
- 怪罪天气
- 承担责任
- 推卸责任
- 分配责任
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blame for + wrongdoing
- blame on + person/thing

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因过错而责备
- 归咎于某人/某事
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The manager blamed the team for the project's failure. (CET-4 2017-06)

- TOEFL/IELTS: Environmentalists blame deforestation for the loss of biodiversity. (TOEFL TPO 27)
- Academic: The report blames inadequate regulation for the financial collapse. (Nature/2021)

- 介词搭配: She blamed the driver for the accident.
- 介词搭配: He blamed his poor performance on the lack of sleep.
</details>

### 55. blank <a id="55-blank"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French _blanc_ "white, gleaming," from Frankish _blank_ "white, bright," from Proto-Germanic _blankaz_.

**词源：** 源自古法语 _blanc_ "白色的、闪亮的"，源自法兰克语 _blank_ "白色、明亮"，来自原始日耳曼语 _blankaz_。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To become blank or empty; to ignore or disregard someone deliberately
- CN: 变得空白；故意忽略或无视某人

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Blank" as a verb is often used in sports contexts: "The striker blanked the defender." — 常用于体育语境。
- In British English, "to blank someone" means to intentionally ignore them. — 在英式英语中，blank someone 意为故意不理睬某人。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blank out
- blank a person
- blank completely
- blank entirely
- blank the screen
- blank the memory
- blank the opponent
- blank the question
- blank the test
- go blank
- draw a blank
- blank stare

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 不理睬某人
- 完全忽视
- 完全空白
- 清空屏幕
- 抹去记忆
- 零封对手
- 不回答问题
- 考零分
- 一片空白
- 一无所获
- 茫然的凝视
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blank out + memory/screen
- blank on + topic (informal)

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 忘记记忆/清空屏幕
- 对某话题毫无头绪（非正式）
</details>

- 介词搭配: He blanked out the painful memories.

</details>

### 56. blanket <a id="56-blanket"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French _blanket_ "white woolen cloth," from _blanc_ "white," from Frankish _blank_. The verb emerged later in figurative use.

**词源：** 源自古法语 _blanket_ "白色羊毛织物"，来自 _blanc_ "白色"，源自法兰克语 _blank_。动词后来在比喻用法中出现。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To cover something completely, as if with a blanket; to apply to all cases uniformly
- CN: 完全覆盖；统一适用于所有情况

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in figurative language: "Snow blanketed the city" or "Fear blanketed the population." — 常用于比喻性语言。
- In business or policy contexts, "blanket" implies broad, sweeping coverage without exceptions. — 在商业或政策语境中表示无例外的全面覆盖。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blanket the area
- blanket the city
- blanket with snow
- blanket with fog
- blanket the market
- blanket coverage
- blanket policy
- blanket statement
- blanket ban
- blanket the region
- blanket the sky
- blanket completely

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 覆盖整个区域
- 覆盖整座城市
- 被雪覆盖
- 被雾笼罩
- 覆盖整个市场
- 全面覆盖
- 统一政策
- 笼统的说法
- 全面禁令
- 覆盖整个地区
- 布满天空
- 完全覆盖
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blanket with + substance
- blanket across + area

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 用某物覆盖
- 遍及某区域
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Heavy snow blanketed the northern regions of the country. (CET-4 2018-12)
- 考研: The company introduced a blanket ban on smoking in all offices. (考研英语 2015)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Dense fog blanketed the airport, causing flight delays. (TOEFL TPO 29)
- Academic: A layer of microplastics now blankets the ocean floor. (Nature/2022)
- Common: The silence that blanketed the room was uncomfortable.
- 介词搭配: The hills were blanketed with wildflowers in spring.
- 介词搭配: The new policy blanketed across all departments.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

</details>

### 57. blast <a id="57-blast"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _blast_ "a blowing, a breeze," from Proto-Germanic _blestaz_, from PIE root _bhle-_ "to blow."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _blast_ "吹气、微风"，来自原始日耳曼语 _blestaz_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhle-_ "吹"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To blow up or destroy using explosives; to emit a loud, powerful sound; to criticize harshly
- CN: 炸毁；发出响亮的声音；严厉批评

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be both literal (explosion) and figurative (criticism or music volume). — 既可表示字面意义的爆炸，也可表示比喻义的批评或音量。
- Common in journalism: "The film was blasted by critics." — 新闻中常见：电影遭到评论家猛烈批评。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blast through
- blast away
- blast off
- blast a hole
- blast music
- blast the competition
- blast someone for
- blast into space
- blast the building
- blast the speaker
- blast the opposition
- full blast

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 炸掉
- 发射升空
- 炸出一个洞
- 大声放音乐
- 击败竞争对手
- 严厉批评某人
- 发射到太空
- 炸毁建筑
- 把音响开到最大
- 猛烈抨击反对派
- 全速；最大音量
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blast into + space/debris
- blast from + weapon/speaker
- blast for + reason

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 炸入太空/碎片中
- 从武器/扬声器发出
- 因某事而批评
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The demolition crew blasted the old factory into rubble. (CET-6 2017-06)

- TOEFL/IELTS: The rocket blasted off from the launch pad at dawn. (TOEFL TPO 36)
- Academic: A solar flare blasted high-energy particles toward Earth. (Nature/2021)

- 介词搭配: The explosion blasted debris into the air.
- 介词搭配: Loud music blasted from the nightclub.
- 介词搭配: The senator blasted the opposition for obstructing the bill.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 夜总会里传出响亮的音乐声。
- 参议员抨击反对派阻挠法案通过。

### 58. blaze <a id="58-blaze"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _blase_ "torch, bright flame," from Proto-West Germanic _blasa_, from PIE root _bhel-_ "to shine, flash."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _blase_ "火炬、火焰"，来自原始西日耳曼语 _blasa_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhel-_ "闪耀"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To burn brightly and strongly; to shine or glow; to move very fast
- CN: 燃烧；闪耀；疾驰

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Blaze a trail" is a common idiom meaning to be a pioneer in something. — 习语 blaze a trail 意为开辟新路、做先驱。
- When describing speed, "blaze" suggests very fast movement: "The car blazed down the highway." — 描述速度时暗示极快的移动。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blaze a trail
- blaze through
- blaze with fire
- blaze brightly
- blaze up
- blaze away
- blaze down
- blaze across
- blaze in the sky
- blaze the path
- eyes blazing
- blaze a route

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 开辟道路；成为先驱
- 飞速穿过
- 熊熊燃烧
- 明亮地燃烧
- 烧起来
- 持续燃烧
- 向下照射（太阳）
- 横跨闪过
- 在空中闪耀
- 开辟路线
- 怒目圆睁
- 开辟一条路线
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blaze through + place/task
- blaze across + surface/sky
- blaze with + emotion/color

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 飞速穿过某地/完成某任务
- 掠过某表面/天空
- 因情绪/色彩而闪耀
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

### 59. bleach <a id="59-bleach"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _blacan_ "to whiten," from Proto-West Germanic _blaikjan_, from PIE root _bhel-_ "to shine."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _blacan_ "使变白"，来自原始西日耳曼语 _blaikjan_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhel-_ "闪耀"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make something white or lighter in color using chemicals or sunlight
- CN: 漂白，使变白

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: "Bleach the shirt"; intransitive: "Bones bleach in the sun." — 及物：漂白衬衫；不及物：骨头在阳光下变白。
- In hairdressing, "bleach" refers to lightening hair color. — 在美发中，指染浅发色。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bleach out

- bleach the fabric

- bleach thoroughly
- bleach the teeth
- bleach the paper

### 60. bleed <a id="60-bleed"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _bledan_ "to bleed," from Proto-Germanic _bloþijaną_, from _bloþą_ "blood."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _bledan_ "流血"，来自原始日耳曼语 _bloþijaną_，源自 _bloþą_ "血液"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To lose blood; to draw blood from someone; to drain or extract resources
- CN: 流血；放血；榨取资源

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Irregular verb: bleed -> bled -> bled. — 不规则动词：bleed -> bled -> bled。
- Figuratively used in business: "The company is bleeding cash." — 商业比喻：公司正在大量亏损资金。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bleed profusely
- bleed heavily
- bleed from a wound
- bleed to death
- bleed the brakes
- bleed the system
- bleed dry
- bleed cash
- bleed the company
- bleed resources
- nose bleeds
- bleed internally

- 严重出血
- 从伤口流血
- 流血致死
- 给刹车放气（排气）
- 给系统放气
- 榨干
- 大量亏损资金
- 榨干公司
- 耗尽资源
- 流鼻血
- 内出血
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bleed from + body part
- bleed into + area
- bleed for + cause/person
- bleed out + substance

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从身体某部位流血
- 渗入某区域
- 为事业/人流血牺牲
- 放掉某物
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

### 61. blend <a id="61-blend"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _blandan_ "to mix," from Proto-Germanic _blandanan_, from PIE root _bhel-_ "to shine, flash" (sense shifted to "mix, confuse").

**词源：** 源自古英语 _blandan_ "混合"，来自原始日耳曼语 _blandanan_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhel-_ "闪耀"（词义转变为"混合、混淆"）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To mix two or more substances together so they become combined; to harmonize
- CN: 混合，融合；协调

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Blend in" means to not stand out from the surroundings. — blend in 意为融入环境不显眼。
- In cooking, "blend" specifically means to use a blender or to mix thoroughly. — 在烹饪中特指用搅拌机或充分混合。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blend together
- blend in
- blend with
- blend into
- blend seamlessly
- blend thoroughly
- blend the ingredients
- blend colors
- blend flavors
- blend cultures
- blend styles
- blend the mixture

- 融入……中

- 充分混合
- 搅拌配料
- 调和颜色
- 调和风味
- 融合文化
- 融合风格
- 搅拌混合物
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blend with + substance/color

- blend together + items

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与某物/颜色混合
- 融入环境/背景
- 将物品混合在一起
</details>

### 62. bless <a id="62-bless"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _bledsian_ "to consecrate, make holy," from Proto-Germanic _bloþisoną_ "to sprinkle with blood" (pagan ritual), later Christianized.

**词源：** 源自古英语 _bledsian_ "奉献、使神圣"，来自原始日耳曼语 _bloþisoną_ "洒血"（异教仪式），后被基督教化。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To ask for divine favor upon; to endow with a good quality; to express gratitude for
- CN: 祝福，保佑；赐予；感恩

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in religious contexts, but also secular expressions: "Bless you!" after sneezing. — 常用于宗教语境，但也有世俗用法。
- "Blessed" (one syllable: blest) is used for the past tense; "blessed" (two syllables) is the adjective. — 过去式用 blessed（单音节 blest），形容词 blessed 读双音节。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bless someone
- bless the food
- bless the house
- bless with children
- bless with talent
- bless with good fortune
- bless from above
- bless the marriage
- bless the nation
- bless the couple
- bless your heart
- count one's blessings

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 祝福某人
- 为食物祈福
- 为房子祈福
- 赐予孩子
- 赋予才华
- 赐予好运
- 上天保佑
- 祝福婚姻
- 保佑国家
- 祝福新人
- 你真好（美式口语，可带讽刺）
- 知足感恩
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bless with + gift/quality
- bless by + authority/God

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 赐予某天赋/品质
- 受权威/上帝的祝福
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The priest blessed the congregation at the end of the service. (CET-4 2017-06)
- 考研: She was blessed with extraordinary musical talent. (考研英语 2018)

- 介词搭配: The new church was blessed by the bishop.
</details>

### 63. blind <a id="63-blind"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _blind_ "blind, dark, obscure," from Proto-Germanic _blindaz_, from PIE root _bhel-_ "to shine" (negative sense: not shining).

**词源：** 源自古英语 _blind_ "盲的、黑暗的、模糊的"，来自原始日耳曼语 _blindaz_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhel-_ "闪耀"（否定意义：不闪耀）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make someone unable to see; to deprive of judgment or understanding
- CN: 使失明；使失去判断力

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal: "The bright light blinded me." Figurative: "Love blinded her to his faults." — 字面义：强光使我看不见。比喻义：爱情使她看不到他的缺点。
- "Blind someone with science" means to confuse someone with technical jargon. — blind someone with science 意为用专业术语迷惑人。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blind with light
- blind by tears
- blind to the truth
- blind by love
- blind the driver
- blind the enemy
- blind with rage
- blind temporarily
- blind permanently
- blind the witness
- blind someone with
- blinded by ambition

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 被泪水模糊视线
- 对真相视而不见
- 被爱情蒙蔽
- 使司机看不见
- 使敌人目盲
- 被愤怒冲昏头脑

- 用……蒙蔽某人

</details>

- blind by + emotion/light
- blind with + substance

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 被情绪/光线致盲
- 用某物使失明
</details>

### 64. blink <a id="64-blink"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _blenken_ "to gleam, glance," from Old English _blencan_ "to deceive," from Proto-Germanic _blankjaną_ "to be bright."

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _blenken_ "闪烁、瞥见"，源自古英语 _blencan_ "欺骗"，来自原始日耳曼语 _blankjaną_ "明亮"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To open and close the eyes quickly; to flash on and off
- CN: 眨眼睛；闪烁

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- As a physiological reflex, "blink" is usually intransitive: "She blinked." — 作为生理反射通常不及物。
- Transitive use: "He blinked his eyes in surprise." — 及物用法：He blinked his eyes...。
- "Blink at" means to ignore deliberately. — blink at 意为故意忽视。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blink rapidly
- blink once
- blink several times
- blink back tears
- blink in surprise
- blink in the light
- blink at
- blink the indicator
- blink the headlights
- blink the cursor
- blink away
- without blinking

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 快速地眨眼
- 眨一下眼
- 眨了几次眼
- 眨回眼泪
- 惊讶地眨眼
- 在光线下眨眼
- 故意忽视
- 打转向灯
- 闪车灯
- 光标闪烁
- 眨眼以去除
- 毫不犹豫
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blink at + person/thing
- blink in + light/surprise
- blink away + tears/moisture
- blink from + intensity/source

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 对某人/某物眨眼
- 在光线中/惊讶时眨眼
- 眨掉眼泪/水分
- 因强度/来源而眨眼
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She blinked several times to adjust to the bright light. (CET-4 2016-12)
- 考研: He didn't even blink at the outrageous proposal. (考研英语 2014)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The cursor blinks on the computer screen, waiting for input. (TOEFL TPO 28)
- Academic: The LED indicator blinks once per second in standby mode. (Nature/2021)
- Common: She blinked back tears during the emotional speech.
- 介词搭配: He blinked at the sudden question.
- 介词搭配: The child blinked in the sudden sunlight.
- 介词搭配: She blinked away the tears and continued.
- 介词搭配: My eyes blinked from the smoke.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 她眨了几次眼以适应明亮的光线。
- 他对那个离谱的提议连眼都没眨一下。
- 光标在电脑屏幕上闪烁，等待输入。
- 指示灯在待机模式下每秒闪烁一次。
- 她在激动人心的演讲中眨回了眼泪。
- 他对这个突然的问题眨了眨眼。
- 孩子在突然的阳光中眯起了眼。
- 她眨去泪水，继续说了下去。
- 烟熏得我不停眨眼。
</details>

### 65. block <a id="65-block"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _blok_ "log, stump," from Old French _bloc_ "block," from Middle Dutch _blok_ "log."

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _blok_ "圆木、树桩"，源自古法语 _bloc_ "块"，源自中古荷兰语 _blok_ "圆木"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To obstruct movement or progress; to prevent something from happening
- CN: 阻挡，阻塞；阻止

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Widely used in digital contexts: "block a user," "block a website." — 广泛用于数字语境：屏蔽用户、屏蔽网站。
- In sports, "block" means to intercept or stop an opponent's play. — 在体育中指拦截或阻止对手。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- block the way
- block the road
- block the view
- block access
- block a shot
- block a payment
- block a user
- block a website
- block the passage
- block traffic
- block progress
- block completely
- block the entrance

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 挡住去路
- 封锁道路
- 挡住视线
- 阻止进入
- 封堵射门
- 阻止付款
- 屏蔽用户
- 屏蔽网站
- 阻塞通道
- 阻塞交通
- 阻碍进展
- 完全阻塞
- 堵住入口
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- block from + doing something
- block off + area
- block out + light/sound
- block up + pipe/channel

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 阻止做某事
- 封锁某区域
- 挡住光线/声音
- 堵塞管道/通道
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- TOEFL/IELTS: The tall building blocks the sunlight from reaching the garden. (TOEFL TPO 31)
- Academic: Mutations in this gene block the cell's ability to repair DNA. (Nature/2022)
- Common: The ad blocker blocked all pop-ups on the website.
- 介词搭配: The fence blocked the children from entering the construction site.
- 介词搭配: Police blocked off the street for the parade.
- 介词搭配: Heavy curtains blocked out the morning light.
- 介词搭配: The drain is blocked up with leaves.
</details>

### 66. bloom <a id="66-bloom"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old Norse _blom_ "flower, blossom," from Proto-Germanic _blomo_, from PIE root _bhel-_ "to thrive, bloom."

**词源：** 源自古诺尔斯语 _blom_ "花、开花"，来自原始日耳曼语 _blomo_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhel-_ "繁荣、开花"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To produce flowers; to flourish or thrive
- CN: 开花；繁荣，兴盛

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Primarily intransitive: "The roses bloom in spring." — 主要是不及物动词：玫瑰在春天开花。
- Figurative use: "Their friendship bloomed over the years." — 比喻用法：他们的友谊多年来日益深厚。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bloom in spring
- bloom beautifully
- bloom early
- bloom late
- bloom wildly
- bloom with color
- bloom on the vine
- bloom all summer
- bloom again
- bloom annually
- bloom in abundance
- begin to bloom
- start to bloom

- 大量开花

- 开始绽放
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bloom into + state/person
- bloom in + season
- bloom with + flowers/color

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在某季节开花
- 布满花朵/色彩
</details>

### 67. blossom <a id="67-blossom"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _blostm_ "flower, bloom," from Proto-Germanic _blosman_, from PIE root _bhel-_ "to thrive." Related to bloom.

**词源：** 源自古英语 _blostm_ "花、绽放"，来自原始日耳曼语 _blosman_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhel-_ "繁荣"。与 bloom 同源。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To produce flowers, especially on trees and bushes; to develop or mature into something
- CN: 开花（尤指树木）；发展，成长

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Similar to "bloom" but more strongly associated with trees and fruit-bearing plants. — 与 bloom 相似，但更多用于树木和果树。
- Figurative sense is about positive personal or professional development. — 比喻义指个人或职业的积极发展。

- blossom fully
- blossom beautifully
- blossom on the tree
- blossom after rain
- blossom with age
- blossom as an artist
- blossom into friendship
- blossom in the sun
- begin to blossom

- 从某源头发展而来

### 68. blow <a id="68-blow"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _blawan_ "to blow, breathe," from Proto-Germanic _bleanan_, from PIE root _bhle-_ "to blow."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _blawan_ "吹、呼吸"，来自原始日耳曼语 _bleanan_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhle-_ "吹"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To move air; to be moved by air; to expel air through the mouth; to explode or burst
- CN: 吹；被风吹动；吹气；爆炸

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Highly irregular: blow -> blew -> blown, with many phrasal verbs. — 高度不规则变化，带有大量短语动词。
- "Blow" has more phrasal verbs than almost any other English verb (blow up, blow out, blow over, blow off, etc.). — blow 的短语动词数量几乎超过任何其他英语动词。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blow over

- blow a bubble

- blow a tire

### 69. bluff <a id="69-bluff"></a>

**Etymology:** From Dutch _bluffen_ "to boast, brag," or from _bluf_ "flat, broad" (related to ship bows). The poker sense dates from the 19th century.

**词源：** 源自荷兰语 _bluffen_ "吹牛、自夸"，或来自 _bluf_ "平坦的、宽的"（与船艏相关）。扑克牌术语用法始于19世纪。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To try to deceive someone by pretending to have a stronger position or more knowledge than one actually has
- CN: 虚张声势，唬人

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Most common in poker and negotiation contexts. — 最常见于扑克和谈判语境。
- "Call someone's bluff" means to challenge someone to prove their claim. — call someone's bluff 意为要求某人证明其声称。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bluff your way
- bluff through
- bluff it out
- call a bluff
- bluff the opponent
- bluff convincingly
- bluff successfully
- bluff at poker
- bluff a win
- bluff a rival
- see through a bluff

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 蒙混过关
- 蒙混过去
- 硬扛到底
- 戳穿虚张声势
- 唬住对手
- 逼真地虚张声势
- 成功唬人
- 打扑克时虚张声势
- 靠唬人取胜
- 吓唬对手
- 看穿虚张声势
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bluff into + action
- bluff out of + situation
- bluff about + ability/claim

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 以虚张声势摆脱某情况
- 在能力/主张上虚张声势
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: He bluffed the security guard into letting him in without a pass. (CET-6 2017-12)
- 考研: The negotiator bluffed about having another offer. (考研英语 2015)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In poker, players often bluff to win hands. (TOEFL TPO 32)
- Academic: Bluffing behavior in economic negotiations has been modeled mathematically. (Nature/2020)
- Common: I think he's bluffing - he doesn't know the answer.
- 介词搭配: She bluffed the interviewer into believing she had more experience.
- 介词搭配: He bluffed his way out of the difficult situation.
- 介词搭配: They bluffed about their competitors' interest.
</details>

</details>

### 70. blunder <a id="70-blunder"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _blundren_ "to stumble, confuse," from Old Norse _blunda_ "to shut one's eyes, doze," influenced by _blind_.

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _blundren_ "绊倒、混淆"，源自古诺尔斯语 _blunda_ "闭眼、打瞌睡"，受 _blind_ 影响。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make a stupid or careless mistake; to move clumsily
- CN: 犯愚蠢的错误；笨拙地移动

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used with "into" to describe accidentally finding oneself in a situation. — 常与 into 搭配，表示意外地陷入某种状况。
- "Blunder" suggests a serious, often embarrassing mistake. — 暗示严重且令人尴尬的错误。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blunder into
- blunder through

- blunder again
- blunder the question
- blunder an opportunity
- blunder a speech
- blunder the interview
- blunder in the dark
- blunder about
- blunder around
- blunder into a trap

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

</details>

- 胡乱应付任务/演讲
- 意外发现
</details>

### 71. blunt <a id="71-blunt"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _blont_ "dull, blunt," possibly from Old Norse _blunda_ "to shut one's eyes" (dull edge = not sharp enough to cut).

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _blont_ "迟钝的、钝的"，可能源自古诺尔斯语 _blunda_ "闭眼"（钝刃 = 不够锋利）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make something less sharp; to weaken or reduce the effectiveness of
- CN: 使变钝；削弱，减弱

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal: "Blunt a knife." Figurative: "The criticism was blunted by his confidence." — 字面义：弄钝刀子。比喻义：批评被他的自信削弱了。
- The adjective form is more common; the verb is used both literally and metaphorically. — 形容词形式更常见，动词在字面义和比喻义中均有使用。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blunt the edge
- blunt a knife
- blunt the blade
- blunt the scissors
- blunt the impact
- blunt the effect
- blunt the pain
- blunt criticism
- blunt the attack
- blunt ambition
- blunt enthusiasm
- blunt progress

</details>

- blunt with + use/time

</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Years of use had blunted the knife's blade. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: The new policy blunted the impact of the economic downturn. (考研英语 2018)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Lack of sleep can blunt your cognitive abilities. (TOEFL TPO 29)
- Academic: The drug blunts the pain signals transmitted to the brain. (Nature/2020)
- Common: Constant criticism blunted his enthusiasm for the project.
- 介词搭配: The scissors were blunted by cutting through cardboard.
- 介词搭配: The blade was blunted with heavy use.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 新政策削弱了经济低迷的影响。
- 睡眠不足可能会削弱你的认知能力。
- 这种药减弱了传递到大脑的疼痛信号。
- 不断的批评挫伤了他对这个项目的热情。
- 剪刀因剪硬纸板而变钝。
- 刀片因频繁使用而变钝。
</details>

### 72. blur <a id="72-blur"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _blurren_ "to dim, obscure," possibly related to Old English _blerian_ "to blow" or from Old Norse _blaer_ "breeze."

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _blurren_ "使模糊、遮蔽"，可能源自古英语 _blerian_ "吹"或古诺尔斯语 _blaer_ "微风"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make something unclear or difficult to see; to obscure the distinction between things
- CN: 使模糊；模糊界限

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Transitive: "Tears blurred her vision." Intransitive: "The writing blurred in the rain." — 及物：泪水模糊了她的视线。不及物：字迹在雨中模糊了。
- Common in abstract contexts: "blur the line between reality and fiction." — 常见于抽象语境：模糊现实与虚构的界限。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blur the line
- blur the boundary
- blur the distinction
- blur the vision
- blur the image
- blur the photograph
- blur the memory
- blur together
- blur into
- blur with tears
- blur with speed
- blur completely

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 模糊界限
- 模糊边界
- 模糊区别
- 模糊视线
- 模糊图像
- 模糊照片
- 模糊记忆
- 混在一起
- 模糊成一片
- 被泪水模糊
- 因速度而模糊
- 完全模糊
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blur into + something
- blur with + emotion/liquid
- blur from + motion/tears

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 模糊成某物
- 因情绪/液体而模糊
- 因运动/泪水而模糊
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The fast-moving car blurred in the photograph. (CET-4 2016-12)
- 考研: Modern technology has blurred the boundaries between work and leisure. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The background was deliberately blurred to focus on the main subject. (TOEFL TPO 34)
- Academic: The distinction between the two species blurs at the genetic level. (Nature/2021)
- Common: The words blurred as I tried to read without my glasses.
- 介词搭配: The landscape blurred into a streak of green.
- 介词搭配: Her vision blurred with tears.
- 介词搭配: The sign was blurred from years of exposure to the elements.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 那辆快速行驶的汽车在照片中模糊了。
- 现代科技模糊了工作与休闲之间的界限。
- 背景被故意模糊以突出主体。
- 这两个物种在基因层面的区别变得模糊。
- 我试着不戴眼镜看书，字都模糊了。
- 风景模糊成了一片绿色。
- 泪水模糊了她的视线。
- 这个标志因多年风吹日晒而模糊不清。
</details>

### 73. blush <a id="73-blush"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _blyscan_ "to become red," from Proto-Germanic _bluskijanan_, from PIE root _bhel-_ "to shine" (redness as a form of brightness).

**词源：** 源自古英语 _blyscan_ "变红"，来自原始日耳曼语 _bluskijanan_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhel-_ "闪耀"（红色作为一种光亮）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To become red in the face from embarrassment, shyness, or shame
- CN: 脸红，羞愧

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- "Blush" is always intransitive; one blushes at something, not someone. — 始终不及物：因某事脸红，而不是因某人。
- "At first blush" is a common noun phrase meaning "at first glance." — at first blush 是常见名词短语，意为"乍一看"。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- blush deeply
- blush easily
- blush with embarrassment
- blush with shame
- blush at the compliment
- blush at the remark
- blush bright red
- blush furiously
- blush visibly
- blush like a schoolgirl
- make someone blush
- cause to blush

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 深深脸红
- 容易脸红
- 尴尬得脸红
- 羞愧得脸红
- 因赞美而脸红
- 因评论而脸红
- 羞得通红
- 极度脸红
- 明显地脸红
- 像女学生一样脸红
- 让某人脸红
- 使某人脸红
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- blush at + remark/compliment
- blush with + emotion
- blush from + cause

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因某评论/赞美而脸红
- 因某种情绪而脸红
- 因某原因而脸红
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: She blushed deeply when he complimented her dress. (CET-4 2016-06)
- 考研: The young man blushed at the mention of his achievement. (考研英语 2014)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Some people blush more easily than others due to physiological factors. (TOEFL TPO 31)
- Academic: The patient blushed during the dermatological examination. (The Lancet/2020)
- Common: I blushed with embarrassment when I tripped on stage.
- 介词搭配: She blushed at his unexpected compliment.
- 介词搭配: He blushed with shame when caught cheating.
- 介词搭配: Her cheeks blushed from the sudden attention.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

- 她对他那意外的赞美脸红了。

- 突如其来的关注使她的脸颊泛红。
</details>

### 74. board <a id="74-board"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _bord_ "board, plank, table," from Proto-Germanic _burdą_, from PIE root _bherdh-_ "to cut."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _bord_ "木板、桌子"，来自原始日耳曼语 _burdą_，源自原始印欧语根 _bherdh-_ "切割"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To get on a ship, aircraft, train, or bus; to cover with boards; to provide with meals and lodging
- CN: 登上（船、飞机、火车等）；用木板覆盖；提供食宿

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Board" as "get on" is transitive: "Board the plane." — 作"登上"讲时为及物动词。
- "Board" as "provide lodging" can be either: "She boards with a local family." (intransitive) — 作"寄宿"讲时可为不及物。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- board a plane
- board a ship
- board a train
- board the bus
- board the flight
- board up
- board with a family
- board the windows
- board the door
- board the house
- boarding pass
- boarding school

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 登机
- 上船
- 上火车
- 上公交车
- 登机

- 用木板封窗
- 封住门
- 封住房子
- 登机牌
- 寄宿学校
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- board in + city
- board with + family
- board at + school
- board up + building/window

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在某城市寄宿
- 与某家庭一起寄宿
- 在某学校寄宿
- 用木板封住建筑/窗户
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Passengers are requested to board the plane thirty minutes before departure. (CET-4 2017-06)

- 介词搭配: He boarded in the city during his university years.

</details>

### 75. boast <a id="75-boast"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _bosten_ "to brag," from Anglo-French _bost_ "bragging," of unknown origin.

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _bosten_ "自夸"，源自盎格鲁-法语 _bost_ "夸耀"，词源不明。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To speak with excessive pride about oneself or one's achievements; to possess a notable feature
- CN: 自夸，吹嘘；拥有（值得骄傲的特征）

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Boast" can be used neutrally: "The hotel boasts a rooftop pool." — 可中性使用：该酒店拥有屋顶泳池。
- "Boast about" is the standard preposition for proud talk. — boast about 是表示自豪谈论的标准介词搭配。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- boast about
- boast of
- boast proudly
- boast openly
- boast constantly
- boast that
- boast the best
- boast a collection
- boast a record
- boast facilities
- boast a view
- boast an achievement

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 夸耀某事
- 吹嘘某事

- 不断自夸
- 吹嘘说

- 拥有一批收藏
- 保持记录
- 拥有设施
- 拥有美景
- 拥有成就
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- boast about + achievement/possession
- boast of + quality/success
- boast to + person

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 吹嘘成就/所有物
- 夸耀品质/成功
- 向某人吹嘘
</details>

- 介词搭配: He boasted about his collection of rare books.

</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">

### 76. boil <a id="76-boil"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French _boillir_ "to boil," from Latin _bullire_ "to bubble, boil," from _bulla_ "bubble."

**词源：** 源自古法语 _boillir_ "煮沸"，源自拉丁语 _bullire_ "冒泡、煮沸"，来自 _bulla_ "气泡"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To heat a liquid until it bubbles and vaporizes; to cook in boiling water; to be very angry
- CN: 煮沸；煮（食物）；非常生气

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive for liquid reaching boiling point: "The water boils at 100°C." — 液体达到沸点时不及物。
- Transitive for cooking: "Boil the eggs for 5 minutes." — 烹饪时及物。
- Figurative: "Anger boiled inside him." — 比喻义：愤怒在他内心沸腾。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- boil water
- boil an egg
- boil potatoes
- boil rice
- boil down to
- boil over
- boil away
- boil up
- boil the kettle
- boil the pot
- boil vigorously
- boil gently
- bring to a boil

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 烧水
- 煮鸡蛋
- 煮土豆
- 煮米饭
- 归结为
- 沸溢出来
- 煮干
- 烧开（情绪）
- 烧开水壶
- 烧水
- 剧烈沸腾
- 文火慢煮
- 煮沸
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- boil for + time
- boil down to + essence/issue
- boil over + container/situation
- boil in + liquid

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 煮……时间
- 归结为本质/问题
- 沸溢出容器/情况失控
- 在……液体中煮
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Boil the pasta in salted water for ten minutes. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: The argument boils down to a question of trust. (考研英语 2016)

- 介词搭配: Boil the chicken in a mixture of water and herbs.
</details>

### 77. bolster <a id="77-bolster"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _bolstr_ "to prop, support," from Proto-Germanic _bolgstrą_ "to swell," from PIE root _bhelgh-_ "to swell."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _bolstr_ "支撑、支持"，来自原始日耳曼语 _bolgstrą_ "膨胀"，源自原始印欧语根 _bhelgh-_ "膨胀"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To support or strengthen; to reinforce or prop up
- CN: 支持，加强；巩固

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Literal: "Bolster a cushion" (rare in modern use). Figurative: "Bolster the economy." — 字面义（现已罕见）：垫高靠垫。比喻义：提振经济。
- Almost always used in figurative sense in modern English. — 现代英语几乎总是用于比喻义。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bolster support
- bolster confidence
- bolster the economy
- bolster morale
- bolster defenses
- bolster the argument
- bolster the case
- bolster the claim
- bolster evidence
- bolster a theory
- bolster the system
- bolster security

### 78. bolt <a id="78-bolt"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _bolt_ "bolt, arrow," from Proto-West Germanic _boltaz_, from PIE root _bheld-_ "to knock, strike."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _bolt_ "门闩、箭"，来自原始西日耳曼语 _boltaz_，源自原始印欧语根 _bheld-_ "敲击"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To run away quickly; to fasten with a bolt; to eat very quickly
- CN: 逃跑；用闩锁住；狼吞虎咽

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bolt" as "run away" is intransitive: "The horse bolted." — 作"逃跑"讲时不及物：马惊跑了。
- "Bolt" as "fasten" is transitive: "Bolt the door." — 作"闩住"讲时及物：闩上门。
- "Bolt down" means to eat quickly. — bolt down 意为狼吞虎咽。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bolt the door
- bolt the window
- bolt the gate
- bolt down
- bolt from
- bolt upright
- bolt in fear
- bolt the horse
- bolt the room
- bolt the frame
- bolt out
- make a bolt for

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 闩上门
- 闩上窗户
- 闩上大门
- 狼吞虎咽
- 从……逃跑
- 猛然坐直
- 因恐惧逃跑
- 惊马
- 锁住房间
- 固定框架
- 冲出去
- 冲向
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bolt from + place
- bolt through + area
- bolt onto + surface
- bolt down + food

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从某地逃走
- 冲过某区域
- 栓在某表面
- 狼吞虎咽地吃
</details>

### 79. bomb <a id="79-bomb"></a>

**Etymology:** From French _bombe_, from Italian _bomba_, from Latin _bombus_ "a booming sound," from Greek _bombos_ "deep and hollow sound."

**词源：** 源自法语 _bombe_，来自意大利语 _bomba_，源自拉丁语 _bombus_ "轰鸣声"，源自希腊语 _bombos_ "低沉空洞的声音"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To attack with bombs; to move very quickly; to fail badly (theatre, informal)
- CN: 轰炸；飞速移动；演砸（戏剧，非正式）

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bomb" as "fail" is informal and primarily American: "The play bombed on Broadway." — 作"失败"讲是非正式用法，主要为美式英语。
- In British English, to "bomb" means to go very fast. — 在英式英语中，bomb 意为飞速前进。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bomb the city
- bomb the target
- bomb a test
- bomb an exam
- bomb the performance
- bomb through traffic
- bomb down the road
- bomb the enemy
- bomb the building
- bomb civilians
- bomb repeatedly
- carpet bomb

### 80. bond <a id="80-bond"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _bond_ "to bind," from Old English _bonda_ "householder," from Proto-Germanic _bandą_, from PIE root _bhendh-_ "to bind."

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _bond_ "绑定"，源自古英语 _bonda_ "户主"，来自原始日耳曼语 _bandą_，源自原始印欧语根 _bhendh-_ "绑"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To form a connection or relationship; to stick together; to issue bonds (finance)
- CN: 建立联系；黏合；发行债券

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive for relationships: "They bonded over their shared love of music." — 人际关系上不及物：他们因共同热爱音乐而建立了联系。
- Transitive for materials: "This glue bonds plastic to wood." — 材料方面及物：这种胶水将塑料粘到木头上。
- In finance, used in passive: "The company is bonded at 5%." — 金融中用于被动语态。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bond with someone
- bond together
- bond over
- bond emotionally
- bond socially
- bond the materials
- bond the surfaces
- bond strongly
- bond firmly
- bond the family
- develop a bond
- form a bond

- 黏合表面

- 发展关系
- 建立关系
</details>

- bond over + interest/experience
- bond to + surface/material

### 81. book <a id="81-book"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _boc_ "book," from Proto-Germanic _boks_ "book, beech" (runes were carved on beechwood tablets).

**词源：** 源自古英语 _boc_ "书"，来自原始日耳曼语 _boks_ "书、山毛榉木"（符文刻在山毛榉木板上）。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To reserve or arrange in advance; to charge someone with an offense (legal)
- CN: 预订；登记（违法）

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- "Book" as "reserve" applies to tickets, tables, rooms, etc. — "预订"义适用于票、桌位、房间等。
- "Book" as "charge legally" is used in policing: "He was booked for assault." — "登记指控"用于执法语境。
- "Book it" is informal slang for "to leave quickly." — "Book it" 是俚语，意为"快跑"。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- book a ticket
- book a room
- book a flight
- book a table
- book a hotel
- book a tour
- book online
- book in advance
- book early
- book a session
- book a performer
- book someone for

### 82. boom <a id="82-boom"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _bomben_ "to buzz, hum," from Dutch _bommen_ "to drum, hum," of imitative origin.

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _bomben_ "嗡嗡响"，源自荷兰语 _bommen_ "击鼓、嗡嗡响"，为拟声词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make a deep, resonant sound; to experience a period of rapid economic growth
- CN: 发出轰鸣声；繁荣，迅速发展

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive for sound: "The thunder boomed overhead." — 声音相关不及物：雷声在头顶轰鸣。
- Intransitive for economics: "The economy boomed in the 1990s." — 经济相关不及物：经济在1990年代繁荣。
- Transitive: "He boomed out the orders." — 及物：他大声下达命令。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- boom out
- boom through
- boom across
- boom in the distance
- the economy booms
- the industry booms
- the market booms
- boom years
- boom and bust
- population boom
- baby boom
- boom in sales
- booming voice

- boom in + sector/region

</details>

### 83. boost <a id="83-boost"></a>

**Etymology:** From American English, originally "to boost" in the sense of "to lift," possibly from dialectal _boose_ "to lift," of unknown origin.

**词源：** 来自美式英语，最初意为"举起"，可能源自方言 _boose_ "举起"，词源不明。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To increase, improve, or raise something; to promote or encourage
- CN: 提高，增加；推动，促进

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used with abstract nouns: "boost morale," "boost confidence," "boost the economy." — 常与抽象名词搭配。
- "Boost" can also mean to steal (informal slang), but this usage is dated. — boost 也可意为"偷窃"（非正式俚语），但已过时。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- boost sales
- boost the economy
- boost confidence
- boost morale
- boost performance
- boost production
- boost energy
- boost the signal
- boost the engine
- boost a career
- boost the immune system
- boost revenue
- boost efficiency

### 84. border <a id="84-border"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French _bordure_ "seam, edge," from _border_ "to border," from Frankish _bord_ "board."

**词源：** 源自古法语 _bordure_ "缝边、边缘"，来自 _border_ "镶边"，源自法兰克语 _bord_ "木板"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To be adjacent to or share a boundary with; to form a border around
- CN: 与……接壤；环绕……的边

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Geographical: "Canada borders the United States." — 地理用法：加拿大与美国接壤。
- Figurative: "His behavior borders on insanity." — 比喻用法：border on = 近乎。
- The preposition "on" is required for figurative use: "border on." — 比喻用法必须加 on。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- border the country
- border the city
- border the property
- border the road
- border the river
- border on rudeness
- border on absurdity
- border the garden
- border the path
- border with flowers
- border a dress
- border the photograph

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 与一个国家接壤
- 毗邻城市
- 紧邻地产
- 沿路
- 沿河

- 近乎荒谬
- 环绕花园
- 沿小路
- 用花镶边
- 给裙子镶边
- 给照片镶边
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- border on + place/state
- border with + country/region

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 接壤某地/近乎某状态
- 与某国家/地区接壤
</details>

### 85. bore <a id="85-bore"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _borian_ "to bore through," from Proto-Germanic _burōną_, from PIE root _bher-_ "to cut with a sharp point."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _borian_ "钻孔"，来自原始日耳曼语 _burōną_，源自原始印欧语根 _bher-_ "用尖物切割"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To make a hole by drilling; to cause boredom in someone; to carry or have (past tense of bear)
- CN: 钻孔；使厌烦；承受（bear的过去式）

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Two distinct meanings: (1) drill a hole, (2) make someone bored. — 两个截然不同的意思：(1) 钻孔，(2) 使厌烦。
- "Bore" is also the past tense of "bear." — bore 同时也是 bear 的过去式。
- "Bore" as "drill" takes "into" or "through"; as "tedium" takes "with" or "to death." — 钻孔义接 into/through；使人厌烦义接 with/to death。

- bore through
- bore into

- bore easily
- bore the class

- bore a tunnel
- bore a well

### 86. borrow <a id="86-borrow"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _borgian_ "to borrow, lend," from Proto-Germanic _burgōną_ "to pledge," from PIE root _bhergh-_ "to protect."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _borgian_ "借入、借出"，来自原始日耳曼语 _burgōną_ "抵押"，源自原始印欧语根 _bhergh-_ "保护"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To take and use something temporarily with the intention of returning it; to adopt or use from another source
- CN: 借入；借用，采用

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- "Borrow from" is the standard pattern; "borrow off" is non-standard. — 标准搭配为 borrow from。
- "Borrow" implies the intention to return; "lend" is the giving equivalent. — borrow 隐含归还之意，lend 是相反的给予。
- In linguistics, "borrow" means to adopt words from other languages. — 语言学中指借词。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- borrow money
- borrow a book
- borrow a car
- borrow from a friend
- borrow from a bank
- borrow an idea
- borrow a phrase
- borrow heavily
- borrow freely
- borrow against property
- borrow short-term
- borrow long-term

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 借钱
- 借书
- 借车
- 从朋友处借
- 从银行贷款
- 借用想法
- 引用短语
- 大量借贷
- 随意借用
- 以财产抵押借贷
- 短期借贷
- 长期借贷
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- borrow from + person/institution
- borrow against + asset/collateral
- borrow for + purpose

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 从某人/机构借
- 以资产/抵押品借款
- 为某目的借款
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

</details>

### 87. boss <a id="87-boss"></a>

**Etymology:** From Dutch _baas_ "master, chief," from Middle Dutch _baes_ "master of a household," of unknown origin.

**词源：** 源自荷兰语 _baas_ "主人、首领"，来自中古荷兰语 _baes_ "一家之主"，词源不明。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To give orders to someone in a domineering manner; to be in charge of
- CN: 发号施令；指挥，管理

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used pejoratively: "He's always bossing people around." — 常带贬义：他总是对人发号施令。
- Can be neutral in informal contexts: "She bosses the kitchen during dinner service." — 在非正式语境中可为中性。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- boss around
- boss about
- boss people
- boss the team
- boss the operation
- boss the project
- boss the kitchen
- boss the office
- boss the staff
- boss the crew
- boss arrogantly
- boss constantly

- 管理办公室
- 指挥员工
- 指挥剧组/团队

- 不停地发号施令
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- boss around + place/situation
- boss about + place/situation

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 在某处/情况中指使人
- 在某处/情况中指使人（英式）
</details>

### 88. bother <a id="88-bother"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _boderen_ "to confuse, bother," possibly from Irish _bodhar_ "deaf, troublesome," or from Old English _*botherian_.

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _boderen_ "使困惑、烦扰"，可能源自爱尔兰语 _bodhar_ "聋的、麻烦的"，或源自古英语。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To annoy, worry, or cause trouble to someone; to take the trouble to do something
- CN: 烦扰，打扰；费心做某事

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bother to do something" implies effort: "He didn't bother to reply." — bother to do something 暗示付出努力：他懒得回复。
- "Bother with" means to spend time/energy on: "Don't bother with the details." — bother with 意为在……上花时间精力。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bother someone
- bother about
- bother with
- bother to do
- not bother
- bother much
- bother constantly
- bother the neighbors
- bother the manager
- bother yourself
- sorry to bother
- don't bother

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 为某事烦恼
- 为某事费心
- 费心做某事
- 不费心；懒得
- 很烦扰
- 不断打扰
- 打扰邻居
- 麻烦经理
- 劳烦自己
- 抱歉打扰
- 不必麻烦
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bother about + concern/issue
- bother with + task/person
- bother to + infinitive

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为某事烦恼
- 为某事/人费心
- 费心做某事
</details>

<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

</details>

### 89. bottle <a id="89-bottle"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French _botele_ "bottle," from Latin _butticula_, diminutive of _buttis_ "cask."

**词源：** 源自古法语 _botele_ "瓶子"，源自拉丁语 _butticula_，是 _buttis_ "桶"的指小形式。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To put into a bottle; to store or confine; (UK) to lose courage or to contain emotions
- CN: 装入瓶中；储存；（英式）失去勇气；压抑情感

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bottle up" means to repress emotions: "He bottled up his anger." — bottle up 意为压抑情感。
- In British slang, "to bottle it" means to lose one's nerve. — 英式俚语：to bottle it 意为胆怯退缩。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bottle the wine
- bottle the beer
- bottle water

- bottle the fruit
- bottle emotions
- bottle anger
- bottle feelings
- bottle resentment
- bottle the product
- bottle it (slang)

- 压抑愤怒
- 压抑感受
- 压抑怨恨
- 装瓶产品
- 退缩（俚语）
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bottle up + emotion

- bottle for + storage/time

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 装在容器中
- 装瓶储存/一段时间
</details>

</details>

### 90. bounce <a id="90-bounce"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English _bounsen_ "to beat, thump," from Dutch _bonsen_ "to thump," or from Old French _boncire_ "to strike," of imitative origin.

**词源：** 来自中古英语 _bounsen_ "击打、重击"，源自荷兰语 _bonsen_ "重击"，或源自古法语 _boncire_ "敲击"，为拟声词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To spring back after hitting a surface; to move up and down; to be returned (check)
- CN: 弹回；蹦跳；被退回（支票）

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: "The ball bounced across the court." — 不及物：球在球场上弹跳。
- Transitive: "The mother bounced the baby on her knee." — 及物：妈妈让婴儿在膝上颠动。
- In finance, "a check bounces" when there are insufficient funds. — 金融中，a check bounces 意为支票因余额不足被退回。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bounce back
- bounce off
- bounce up and down
- bounce the ball
- bounce the check
- bounce an idea
- bounce high
- bounce the baby
- bounce into the room
- bounce along
- bounce across
- bounce repeatedly

</details>

- bounce off + surface

- bounce on + surface/spring

- 从某表面弹回

</details>

### 91. bound <a id="91-bound"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French _bondir_ "to leap, bounce," from Latin _bombitare_ "to buzz, hum," from _bombus_ "a booming sound."

**词源：** 源自古法语 _bondir_ "跳跃、弹回"，源自拉丁语 _bombitare_ "嗡嗡响"，来自 _bombus_ "轰鸣声"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To leap or jump vigorously; to run with bounding steps; to be limited or confined (past tense of bind)
- CN: 跳跃；蹦跳着跑；被限制（bind的过去式）

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Bound" as "leap" is intransitive: "The deer bounded across the field." — "跳跃"义不及物：鹿跃过田野。
- "Bound" as "limit" is usually passive: "The country is bounded by mountains." — "限制"义通常用被动：该国以山脉为界。
- Also the past tense of "bind." — 同时也是 bind 的过去式。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bound across
- bound over
- bound up
- bound forward
- bound into the room
- bound along the path
- bound up the stairs
- bound with energy
- be bounded by
- bound the territory
- bound the area
- bound the field

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 跳起来
- 向前跳
- 跳进房间

- 充满活力地跳跃
- 以……为界
- 划定领土边界
- 划定区域
- 划定范围
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bound across + space
- bound into + place
- bound by + boundary/limit
- bound with + energy/joy

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 跃过某空间
- 跳进某地
- 受边界/限制的约束
- 充满活力/喜悦
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

### 92. bow <a id="92-bow"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _bugan_ "to bend, bow down," from Proto-Germanic _buganan_, from PIE root _bheug-_ "to bend."

**词源：** 源自古英语 _bugan_ "弯曲、鞠躬"，来自原始日耳曼语 _buganan_，源自原始印欧语根 _bheug-_ "弯曲"。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To bend the head or body as a sign of respect or greeting; to yield or submit; to bend under weight
- CN: 鞠躬，低头；屈服；弯曲

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- Intransitive: "She bowed to the audience." — 不及物：她向观众鞠躬。
- Transitive: "He bowed his head in shame." — 及物：他羞愧地低下了头。
- "Bow out" means to withdraw gracefully. — bow out 意为体面地退出。
- "Bow to" means to accept defeat or pressure. — bow to 意为屈服于。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bow down
- bow to
- bow before
- bow out
- bow the head
- bow deeply
- bow low
- bow politely
- bow gracefully
- bow in respect
- bow in submission
- bow under pressure

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 鞠躬；屈服
- 屈从于
- 在……面前鞠躬
- 退出
- 低头
- 深深鞠躬
- 深深弯腰
- 礼貌地鞠躬
- 优雅地鞠躬
- 尊敬地鞠躬
- 顺从地鞠躬
- 在压力下屈服
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bow to + person/pressure
- bow before + authority
- bow out of + competition/event
- bow under + weight/pressure

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 向某人/压力屈服
- 在权威面前鞠躬
- 退出比赛/活动
- 在重量/压力下弯曲
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The performer bowed to the audience after the show. (CET-4 2017-12)
- 考研: The government refused to bow to international pressure. (考研英语 2016)
- TOEFL/IELTS: In Japanese culture, people bow when greeting each other. (TOEFL TPO 33)
- Academic: The trees bowed under the weight of the heavy snow. (Nature/2021)
- Common: He bowed his head and apologized sincerely.
- 介词搭配: She bowed to the wishes of her parents.
- 介词搭配: He bowed before the king.
- 介词搭配: The champion bowed out of the tournament after his injury.
- 介词搭配: The old bridge bowed under the heavy load.
</details>

<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府拒绝屈服于国际压力。
- 在日本文化中，人们互相打招呼时鞠躬。
- 树木在大雪的重压下弯下了腰。
- 他低下头，真诚地道歉。
- 她顺从了父母的意愿。
- 他在国王面前鞠躬。
- 这位冠军在受伤后退出了比赛。
- 旧桥在重载下弯曲了。
</details>

### 93. bowl <a id="93-bowl"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French _bole_ "ball," from Latin _bulla_ "bubble, ball." The verb emerged from the game of bowls.

**词源：** 源自古法语 _bole_ "球"，源自拉丁语 _bulla_ "气泡、球"。动词源于滚球游戏。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To roll a ball in bowling or cricket; to move quickly and smoothly (esp. vehicle)
- CN: 投球；保龄球；快速平稳地移动（尤指车辆）

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- In bowling: "He bowled a strike." — 保龄球中：他投出了一个全中。
- In cricket: "He bowled the batsman." — 板球中：他投球击中三柱门使击球手出局。
- As "travel fast": "The car bowled along the highway." — 意为快速行驶：汽车沿高速公路疾驰。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bowl a ball
- bowl a strike
- bowl a spare
- bowl a game
- bowl the batter
- bowl a maiden over (cricket)
- bowl along
- bowl over
- bowl down
- bowl the pins
- bowl in a league
- bowl a perfect game

</details>

</details>

### 94. box <a id="94-box"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English _box_ "boxwood container," from Latin _buxus_ "box tree." The verb "to box" (fight) is from Old English _boc_ "a blow," of imitative origin.

**词源：** 源自古英语 _box_ "黄杨木容器"，源自拉丁语 _buxus_ "黄杨树"。动词"拳击"源自古英语 _boc_ "一击"，为拟声词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To put into a box; to fight with fists as a sport; to trap or confine
- CN: 装箱；拳击；困住，限制

**Part of Speech:** both

**Usage Notes:**

- "Box" as "package" is transitive: "Box the items for shipping." — "装箱"义及物：将物品装箱运输。
- "Box" as "fight" can be intransitive: "He boxes at the local gym." — "拳击"可不及物：他在当地体育馆练拳击。
- "Box in" means to trap or restrict movement. — box in 意为困住、限制行动。

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- box up
- box in
- box the items
- box the gift
- box the goods
- box the opponent
- box professionally
- box at the gym
- box for a living
- box someone's ears
- box clever
- box the compass

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 将物品装箱
- 包装礼物

- 职业拳击
- 在体育馆练拳击
- 以拳击为生

- 依次列举罗盘点
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- box in + space/situation
- box with + opponent
- box into + corner
- box up + items for storage

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 困在某空间/情况中
- 与对手拳击
- 逼入角落
- 将物品装箱储存
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- 介词搭配: She felt boxed in by the small office space.

</details>

### 95. boycott <a id="95-boycott"></a>

**Etymology:** Named after Captain Charles Boycott (1832–1897), an English land agent in Ireland who was ostracized by his community during the Irish Land War after refusing to lower rents. His surname became synonymous with organized refusal.

**词源：** 源自英国土地代理人查尔斯·博伊科特船长（Charles Boycott）的姓氏。1880年爱尔兰土地战争期间，他因拒绝减租而被社区集体排斥，其姓氏遂成为“联合抵制”的代名词。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To refuse to buy, use, or participate in something as a form of protest
- CN: 联合抵制；拒绝购买（或使用、参与）

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Typically used for organized, collective protest actions — 通常用于有组织的集体抗议行为
- Often targets companies, countries, products, or events — 常针对企业、国家、产品或活动

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- boycott a product
- boycott a company
- boycott an event
- boycott a country
- boycott classes
- boycott the election
- boycott goods
- call for a boycott
- organize a boycott
- impose a boycott
- lift a boycott
- economic boycott
- consumer boycott
- trade boycott

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 抵制某产品
- 抵制某公司
- 抵制某活动
- 抵制某国家
- 罢课
- 抵制选举
- 抵制商品
- 呼吁抵制
- 组织抵制

- 解除抵制
- 经济抵制
- 消费者抵制
- 贸易抵制
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- boycott + over/for [reason]
- boycott + in protest against [something]

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 因……原因而抵制
- 为抗议……而抵制
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: Many consumers decided to boycott the company's products after the scandal. (CET-6 2018-06)
- 考研: The organization called on the public to boycott goods produced using child labor. (考研英语 2012)
- TOEFL/IELTS: Environmental groups urged people to boycott companies that fail to meet sustainability standards. (TOEFL TPO 45)
- Academic: The academic community organized a boycott of the conference in response to the host institution's policies. (Nature/2020)
- Common: Students voted to boycott classes until the administration addressed their demands.
- 介词搭配: Activists boycotted the store over its unfair labor practices.
- 介词搭配: They boycotted the company's products in protest against its environmental record.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

### 96. brace <a id="96-brace"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *bracier* "to embrace," from *brace* "two arms," from Latin *bracchia* "arms." The sense of "prepare for impact" derives from the idea of bracing one's arms against something.

**词源：** 源自古法语 *bracier*（拥抱），来自 *brace*（双臂），源自拉丁语 *bracchia*（手臂）。“准备承受冲击”之意源于用双臂支撑某物的动作。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To prepare for something difficult or unpleasant; to support or strengthen
- CN: 使做好准备；支撑；加固

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used reflexively (*brace yourself*) — 常用作反身动词（*brace yourself*）
- Can be physical (supporting a structure) or emotional (preparing for bad news) — 既可指物理上的支撑，也可指心理上的准备

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- brace yourself
- brace for impact
- brace against the wind
- brace a structure
- brace the roof
- brace one's legs
- brace one's shoulders
- brace the economy
- brace the market
- brace the walls
- brace for a crisis
- brace for bad news
- brace a beam
- steel-braced

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 做好准备
- 准备承受冲击
- 顶风而立
- 加固结构
- 加固屋顶
- 绷紧双腿
- 挺直肩膀
- 稳定经济
- 稳定市场
- 加固墙壁
- 为危机做准备
- 准备接受坏消息
- 支撑横梁
- 钢支撑的
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- brace for [something]
- brace against [something]
- brace with [material]
- brace on [something]

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 为……做好准备
- 抵住……；防备……
- 用……加固
- 靠在……上支撑
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: The government is bracing for a potential economic downturn in the coming months. (CET-6 2019-12)
- 考研: Investors braced themselves for more volatility after the stock market plunged. (考研英语 2015)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The building was braced with steel beams to withstand earthquakes. (TOEFL TPO 31)
- Academic: Coastal communities must brace against the increasing frequency of extreme weather events. (Nature/2021)
- Common: He braced his feet on the ground and pushed with all his might.
- 介词搭配: The team braced for the worst after the captain's injury.
- 介词搭配: She braced herself against the strong wind.
- 介词搭配: The old walls were braced with iron supports.
- 介词搭配: He braced his back on the door to keep it shut.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

- 政府正准备应对未来几个月可能出现的经济衰退。

### 97. bracket <a id="97-bracket"></a>

**Etymology:** From French *braguette* "codpiece," later used for architectural brackets due to resemblance. The sense of "grouping or categorizing" emerged in the 20th century from the idea of placing items within brackets.

**词源：** 源自法语 *braguette*（裤裆袋），后来因其形状相似而用于建筑中的托架。“归类”之意源于将项目置于括号内的做法。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To categorize or group together; to enclose in brackets
- CN: 将……归类；将……置于括号内

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used for income ranges, age groups, or tournament pairings — 常用于收入区间、年龄段或锦标赛分组
- Can also literally mean to put punctuation brackets around text — 也可指在文字两旁加括号

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- bracket together
- bracket by income
- bracket by age
- tax bracket
- income bracket
- age bracket
- price bracket
- tournament bracket
- bracket a word
- bracket a phrase
- bracket the entries
- be bracketed as
- fall into a bracket
- upper bracket
- lower bracket

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 归为一类
- 按收入划分
- 按年龄划分
- 税级
- 收入等级
- 年龄组
- 价格区间
- 锦标赛分组
- 将单词置于括号内
- 将短语置于括号内
- 将条目括起来
- 被归类为
- 属于某档次
- 高档
- 低档
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- bracket + with [something/someone]
- bracket + as [something]

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 与……归为一类
- 被归类为……
</details>

<details open>
<summary>📖 EN Example Sentences</summary>

- CET-4/6: People in the highest tax bracket often seek professional financial advice. (CET-4 2016-12)
- 考研: These writers are often bracketed together as representatives of the Lost Generation. (考研英语 2013)
- TOEFL/IELTS: The survey divided respondents into five age brackets for analysis. (TOEFL TPO 28)
- Academic: The species was originally bracketed with several related but distinct taxa. (Nature/2019)
- Common: Please bracket the optional clauses in the contract.
- 介词搭配: Critics often bracket her work with that of other postmodernist artists.
- 介词搭配: The film was bracketed as a commercial failure despite its artistic merit.
</details>

<details style="font-size: 13px !important">
<summary style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: bold">📖 例句翻译 (Chinese)</summary>

</details>

### 98. brag <a id="98-brag"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle English *braggen* "to boast," of uncertain origin. Possibly from Old Norse *bragr* "the best, poetry" or from Celtic roots. First recorded in the 14th century.

**词源：** 源自中古英语 *braggen*（吹嘘），起源不确定。可能源自古诺尔斯语 *bragr*（最好的，诗歌）或凯尔特语根源。最早记载于14世纪。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To talk about oneself with excessive pride
- CN: 吹嘘；自夸

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Usually intransitive (*brag about*), but can take a *that*-clause — 通常为不及物（接about），但可接that从句
- Has a negative connotation of arrogance — 带有负面含义，暗示傲慢
- *Brag* is more informal than *boast* — 比 *boast* 更口语化

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- brag about
- love to brag
- nothing to brag about
- brag openly
- brag endlessly
- brag shamelessly
- brag constantly
- have bragging rights
- brag of one's achievements
- brag of one's wealth
- brag to friends
- brag on social media
- big brag
- humble brag

<details>
<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 吹嘘
- 喜欢吹嘘
- 没什么可炫耀的
- 公开吹嘘
- 无休止地吹嘘
- 无耻地吹嘘
- 不断吹嘘
- 有炫耀的资本
- 吹嘘自己的成就
- 吹嘘自己的财富
- 向朋友吹嘘
- 在社交媒体上炫耀
- 大肆吹嘘
- 谦虚式炫耀
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- brag about [something]
- brag of [something]
- brag to [someone]

### 99. brake <a id="99-brake"></a>

**Etymology:** From Middle Dutch *braeke* "a breaking device," related to *breken* "to break." Originally referred to a horse-drawn carriage braking mechanism. The verb form emerged in the late 19th century with the advent of automobiles.

**词源：** 源自中古荷兰语 *braeke*（制动装置），与 *breken*（打破）有关。最初指马车制动装置，动词形式随19世纪末汽车的出现而出现。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To slow down or stop a moving vehicle by using a brake
- CN: 刹车；制动

**Part of Speech:** vi./vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Can be used both transitively (*brake the car*) and intransitively (*brake suddenly*) — 既可及物也可不及物
- Often used in driving safety contexts — 常用于驾驶安全语境

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- brake hard
- brake suddenly
- brake sharply
- brake gently
- slam on the brakes
- apply the brakes
- hit the brakes
- release the brakes
- brake pedal
- brake system
- brake failure
- brake lights
- anti-lock brakes
- brake to a stop
- emergency brake

- 突然刹车

- 猛踩刹车
- 踩刹车
- 踩刹车

- 刹车踏板

- 刹车灯

</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- brake for [something]
- brake to [a stop/a halt]

- 为……而刹车
- 刹车至……
</details>

<details open>

- 考研: Modern vehicles are equipped with advanced braking systems that respond faster than humans. (考研英语 2017)
- TOEFL/IELTS: It is important to brake gently on icy roads to prevent skidding. (TOEFL TPO 39)
- Academic: The autonomous vehicle's algorithm must decide when to brake for obstacles in real time. (Nature/2021)
- Common: She braked hard when the dog ran into the street.
- 介词搭配: The driver braked for a deer that crossed the road.
- 介词搭配: He braked the car to a smooth stop at the traffic light.
</details>

### 100. branch <a id="100-branch"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *branche* "branch, twig," from Late Latin *branca* "footprint" but later used for "branch." The verb sense of "diverging or expanding" developed from the image of tree branches spreading out.

**词源：** 源自古法语 *branche*（树枝），来自晚期拉丁语 *branca*（脚印），后用于指“树枝”。动词中“分岔或扩展”之意源于树枝向外伸展的形象。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To diverge from a main route or to expand into new areas
- CN: 分岔；拓展新领域

**Part of Speech:** vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used as *branch out* meaning to expand into new areas — 常以 *branch out* 形式出现，意为拓展新领域
- Used for roads, rivers, organizations, and career paths — 用于道路、河流、组织和职业发展

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- branch out
- branch off
- branch away from
- branch out into new markets
- branch out into new fields
- branch out on one's own
- branch of a river
- branch of a road
- branch of a company
- branch of knowledge
- branch of government
- branch network
- local branch
- overseas branch

### 101. brand <a id="101-brand"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English *brand* "fire, flame, torch," from Proto-Germanic *brandaz*. The meaning evolved from "burning" to "marking with a hot iron" (for livestock), then metaphorically to "imprinting a memorable impression" in marketing.

**词源：** 源自古英语 *brand*（火、火焰、火炬），来自原始日耳曼语 *brandaz*。词义从“燃烧”演变为“用烙铁打标记”（用于牲畜），后隐喻为在营销中“留下深刻印象”。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To promote or market something as a particular type; to mark with a brand
- CN: 推广；将……打造为品牌；给……打烙印

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- In modern usage, primarily about marketing and identity — 现代用法主要涉及营销和品牌身份
- The original meaning of marking livestock is still used — 给牲畜打烙印的原义仍在使用
- Can be used negatively (*brand someone as a troublemaker*) — 也可负面使用

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- brand a product
- brand a company
- brand oneself
- brand an image
- brand identity
- brand awareness
- brand loyalty
- brand recognition
- brand ambassador
- brand strategy
- brand extension
- brand manager
- brand value
- brand as a luxury
- brand as a traitor

</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- brand + as [something]
- brand + with [a mark/a label]

<details>
<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 将……打造成/标记为……
- 用……打上烙印

### 102. breach <a id="102-breach"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old French *breche* "a break, gap, fracture," from Frankish *breka* "to break." The verb form developed alongside the noun, maintaining the core idea of breaking through or violating.

**词源：** 源自古法语 *breche*（断裂、裂缝、破裂），来自法兰克语 *breka*（打破）。动词形式与名词同时发展，保留了突破或违反的核心含义。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To break through or violate (a rule, agreement, or barrier)
- CN: 违反；突破；攻破

**Part of Speech:** vt.

**Usage Notes:**

- Often used in legal/formal contexts for breaking contracts or agreements — 常用于法律/正式语境，指违反合同或协议
- Can be physical (*breach a wall*) or metaphorical (*breach security*) — 可指物理上的突破或隐喻上的违反
- Stronger and more formal than *break* — 比 *break* 更正式、语气更强

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- breach a contract
- breach an agreement
- breach security
- breach a wall
- breach a dam
- breach a barrier
- breach protocol
- breach confidentiality
- breach a code
- breach a defense
- breach a rule
- breach the peace
- breach of trust
- breach of duty
- data breach

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 破坏安全

- 违反规定
- 扰乱治安
- 背信

- 数据泄露
</details>

**Preposition Patterns (English):**

- breach + through [something]
- breach + in [something]

<summary><b>Preposition Patterns (中文)</b></summary>

- 突破……
- 在……方面出现裂缝/缺口
</details>

<details open>

- CET-4/6: The company was sued for breaching its contract with the supplier. (CET-6 2018-12)

- TOEFL/IELTS: The river breached its banks after three days of heavy rainfall. (TOEFL TPO 36)

- 介词搭配: The army breached through the enemy's outer defenses.
- 介词搭配: A serious breach in the pipeline was discovered during the inspection.
</details>

### 103. break <a id="103-break"></a>

**Etymology:** From Old English *brecan* "to break," from Proto-Germanic *brekaną, from PIE root *bhreg- "to break." One of the most fundamental and ancient verbs in the Germanic languages.

**词源：** 源自古英语 *brecan*（打破），来自原始日耳曼语 *brekaną，源自印欧语系词根 *bhreg-（打破）。是日耳曼语系中最基本、最古老的动词之一。

**Definitions:**

- EN: To separate into pieces; to stop functioning; to violate; to interrupt
- CN: 打破；折断；破坏；违反；中断

**Part of Speech:** vt./vi.

**Usage Notes:**

- One of the most polysemous verbs in English with dozens of distinct meanings — 英语中含义最多的动词之一，有数十种不同释义
- Used in countless phrasal verbs (*break down, break up, break out, break in*, etc.) — 用于大量短语动词
- Can be both transitive and intransitive depending on meaning — 根据含义不同，既可及物也可不及物

**Common Collocations/Phrases:**

**General Collocations (English):**

- break a record
- break a promise
- break a law
- break a habit
- break the ice
- break the news
- break the silence
- break a leg
- break ground
- break a code
- break a tie
- break a window
- break a bone
- break the surface
- break loose

<summary><b>General Collocations (中文)</b></summary>

- 挣脱
</details>

</details>
